### [1] [Child-Oriented AIGC Video Risk Reviewing: A Benchmark and Knowledge-Supported Iterative Reasoning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22715)
*Lewen Mi,Manyi Li,Yuling Sun,Yufan Zhang,Yuxin Shi,Yulong Bian,Xiangxian Li,Juan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对儿童观看AIGC视频的安全问题，构建了包含605个真实视频的CAVSR基准数据集和6大类26细粒度的分层风险分类体系，并提出了一种知识与经验增强的视频审查框架QVRS-E，通过多智能体协作与专业知识结合，显著提升了面向儿童的AIGC视频风险识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频安全研究主要从成人视角设计通用违规检测，缺乏针对AIGC视频中儿童可能遇到的细粒度、隐式且依赖上下文的特定风险的识别能力。

Method: 构建CAVSR基准数据集（605个真实视频）和6大类26细粒度的分层风险分类体系；提出QVRS-E框架，结合多智能体协作与专家及经验知识，支持针对性证据获取和基于事实的审查决策。

Result: 实验表明，QVRS-E方法显著提升了与视觉语言模型集成的儿童相关风险审查效果，并生成了更稳健的审查报告。

Conclusion: 面向儿童的AIGC视频审查需要专门的基准和细粒度风险分类体系，知识与经验增强的多智能体协作框架能有效提升审查性能。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Artificial Intelligence-generated content (AIGC) is reshaping video production and circulation, exposing children to an increasing volume of AIGC videos. Unlike traditionally produced videos, AIGC videos often exhibit greater uncertainty in visual details, narrative coherence, and content expression, which may introduce developmentally inappropriate risks for children. However, existing video safety research is largely designed for general violation detection from an adult perspective and remains insufficient for identifying the fine-grained, implicit, and context-dependent risks that children may encounter when viewing AIGC videos. To address this gap, we study child-oriented AIGC video reviewing, making three contributions. First, we construct CAVSR, a benchmark of 605 real-world videos collected from multiple platforms, and develop a hierarchical risk taxonomy comprising 6 top-level categories and 26 fine-grained labels to support systematic evaluation of children's viewing risks. Second, we propose QVRS-E, a knowledge- and experience-augmented video reviewing framework that combines multi-agent collaboration with expert and experiential knowledge to support targeted evidence acquisition and fact-grounded reviewing decisions. Third, extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly enhances the reviewing of child-related risks integrated with vision-language models, and yields more robust review reports.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Appearance: A Multi-cue Framework and Large-scale Benchmark for Pedestrian Association and Tracking on Mobile Aerial-Ground Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23803)
*Ruiqi Wu,Bingliang Jiao,Ruize Han,Hangzheng Yu,Xunkai Jiang,Shining Wang,Yuanqi Hu,Wenxuan Wang,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视角鲁棒的特征统一框架FUSION，通过多线索自适应组合与在线多视角特征同步模块，解决多视角多目标关联与跟踪中视角变化导致的形变问题，并构建了包含50.49万帧、7个摄像头、超过730万标注框的大规模基准数据集RealMvMoAT，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角多目标关联与跟踪面临频繁的视角变化导致的外观形变问题，这破坏了跨视角关联和时序跟踪的性能，现有方法对此缺乏有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出FUSION框架：1）多线索自适应组合模块，自适应整合视角不变线索与外观特征以增强跨视角关联；2）在线多视角特征同步模块，聚合历史帧和跨视角帧的行人特征以实现时序一致的跟踪。同时构建大规模基准数据集RealMvMoAT，包含7个摄像头（5个无人机视角+2个地面视角）在10个场景中的50.49万帧数据。

Result: 在RealMvMoAT和六个公开基准数据集上，FUSION达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: FUSION框架有效解决了多视角场景下视角变化带来的外观形变问题，RealMvMoAT作为目前最大的MvMoAT数据集，其规模、视角多样性、平台运动复杂度和真实轨迹为未来研究提供了全面的资源。

Abstract: Multi-view Multi-object Association and Tracking (MvMoAT) associates objects across camera views and tracks them over time, supporting identity persistence and forensic trajectory reconstruction in multi-platform cooperative perception. Unlike conventional multiple object tracking, MvMoAT faces frequent viewpoint shifts that distort appearance and undermine cross-view association and temporal tracking. We propose FUSION, a viewpoint-robust Feature Unification framework for multi-view aSsociation and IdentificatiON. Its Multi-cue Adaptive Combination (MAC) module adaptively integrates viewpoint-invariant cues with appearance features to improve cross-view association, while Online Multi-view Feature Synchronization (OMFS) aggregates pedestrian features across historical and cross-view frames for temporally consistent tracking. We also introduce RealMvMoAT, a large-scale benchmark featuring substantial inter- and intra-camera viewpoint variation. It contains 504.9K frames from 7 cameras (5 UAV and 2 ground views) across 10 scenes, with over 7.3M identity-labeled bounding boxes. All cameras exhibit random and substantial motion. To the best of our knowledge, RealMvMoAT is the largest MvMoAT dataset to date. Its scale, viewpoint diversity, complex platform motion, and realistic trajectories provide a comprehensive resource for future research. Experiments on RealMvMoAT and six public benchmarks show that FUSION achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [3] [DINOv3-MIL: Per-Kidney Multi-Label Tumour and Cyst Detection from Foundation-Model Patch Tokens on KiTS23](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22687)
*Vishalakshi M,Sahil Sharma,Pramod Kumar P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在肾脏肿瘤/囊肿检测任务中，使用DINOv2 ViT-H/16+冻结特征，比较三种聚合器（CLS线性探针、门控注意力MIL、ProtoViT原型头），发现注意力MIL性能最优（肿瘤AUROC 0.74，囊肿AUROC 0.80），注意力在病灶区域富集7.5-9.8倍，但原型头在囊肿检测上失败（AUROC 0.51），揭示了大规模标记尺度下可解释性与性能之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础视觉模型从自然图像迁移到医学任务无需领域预训练，但体素级分类需要聚合大量图像块标记，而聚合器的选择限制了模型的可解释性，因此需要比较不同聚合器在冻结特征上的表现。

Method: 在KiTS23数据集（966个肾脏，n=97测试）上，使用相同的冻结DINOv3 ViT-H/16+特征，比较三种聚合器：（1）CLS标记线性探针；（2）基于55,296个图像块标记的门控注意力多实例学习（MIL）；（3）ProtoViT原型头。

Result: 注意力MIL在肿瘤检测（AUROC 0.74，95% CI 0.64-0.83）和囊肿检测（AUROC 0.80，95% CI 0.70-0.88）中均达到最高性能，注意力在标注病灶区域内富集7.5-9.8倍。原型头在囊肿检测上AUROC仅为0.51，未能有效迁移。

Conclusion: 注意力MIL在性能和可解释性之间取得较好平衡，而原型头在大规模标记尺度下存在可解释性与性能之间的显著权衡，无法有效迁移到某些任务。

Abstract: Foundation vision models trained on natural images transfer to medical tasks without domain pre-training, but volumetric classification requires aggregating tens of thousands of patch tokens per study, and the aggregator constrains how the resulting model can be interpreted. We compare three aggregators on identical frozen DINOv3 ViT-H/16+ features for renal tumour/cyst detection on KiTS23 (966 kidneys; n=97 test): a CLS-token linear probe, gated attention multiple instance learning (MIL) over 55,296 patch tokens, and a prototype head following ProtoViT. Attention MIL achieves the highest AUROC for tumour (0.74, 95% CI 0.64-0.83) and cyst (0.80, 0.70-0.88), with attention enriched 7.5-9.8x over chance within annotated lesions. The prototype head does not transfer to cyst detection (AUROC 0.51), exposing an interpretability-performance trade-off at this token scale.

</details>


### [4] [MegaSlide-DiT: Memory-Centric Adaptation and Deformable Local Attention for Efficient Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22696)
*Jiacheng Liu,Jason Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MegaSlide-DiT 提出了一种系统级方案，通过将模型状态驻留在主机内存、仅流式传输瞬态分片到 GPU，并采用 3D 可变形滑动注意力替代二次复杂度自注意力，实现在单张 H200 GPU（搭配 1.5TB 主机内存）上适配 105B 参数的视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频扩散模型（基于 DiT）面临两大障碍：参数内存（千亿参数需超 TB 持久状态）和激活内存（时空自注意力呈序列长度平方增长），使得单工作站无法适配大规模生成模型，亟需系统级解决方案。

Method: 1) 模型状态卸载：将所有持久权重、主权重和优化器状态保留在主机内存（1.5TB RAM）中，仅将瞬态分片按需流式传输到 GPU。2) 3D 可变形滑动注意力（3D-DSA）：一种运动自适应的局部注意力算子，将内存和计算复杂度从二次降为线性。

Result: 在单张 H200 GPU 上成功适配 105B 参数的 DiT 模型；提供了详细的内存核算、执行轨迹和评估结果来验证设计有效性。

Conclusion: MegaSlide-DiT 不声称在单 GPU 上从头训练 105B 模型，也不神奇解决带宽限制，而是为在高端工作站上对大规模视频扩散模型进行全参数微调提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: High-resolution video diffusion models built on Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) deliver strong fidelity but quickly exhaust the memory budget of a single workstation. A 100 billion-plus parameter DiT easily requires over a terabyte of persistent state, while naive spatiotemporal self-attention grows quadratically in sequence length. These two walls -- parameter memory and activation memory -- prevent researchers from adapting massive generative models without large GPU clusters. We revisit this problem from a systems perspective and introduce MegaSlide-DiT, a prototype that demonstrates how a pre-trained 105B DiT can be adapted on a single H200 GPU with 1.5 TB of host RAM. Our key insight is that the GPU need not own the model state: all persistent weights, master weights and optimizer moments remain in host memory, while only transient shards are streamed to the GPU on demand. Simultaneously, we replace quadratic global attention with 3D Deformable Slide Attention (3D-DSA), a motion-adaptive local attention operator that reduces both memory and computational complexity to linear in the sequence length. We report detailed memory accounting, execution traces and evaluation results to substantiate our design. MegaSlide-DiT does not claim to train a 105B model from scratch on a single GPU, nor does it magically solve bandwidth limits; rather, it offers a pragmatic path for full-parameter adaptation of massive video diffusion models on high-end workstations.

</details>


### [5] [FogDrive: A Multi-Modal Synthetic Driving Dataset for Perception under Graded Fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22698)
*Vansh Panwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FogDrive是一个严格校准的多模态自动驾驶数据集，包含660个场景（约13.3万帧），覆盖4种相机、LiDAR、语义LiDAR和雷达，在三种能见度级别（160m、100m、50m）下模拟物理一致的雾。每个场景有四种匹配变体（无雾+三级雾）及交叉校准的2D/3D边界框。基于此数据集，研究发现训练时混合多密度雾可改善3D检测框几何质量，而PSNR/SSIM等图像质量指标与下游检测性能相关性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集缺乏系统性的多模态对齐来评估鲁棒传感器融合：真实天气数据集存在采集不可控、条件单一且未校准的问题；合成数据集要么只针对相机去雾，要么缺乏成对的无雾-有雾结构以评估"去雾-检测"流程。

Method: 基于CARLA模拟器构建，包含660个场景（日/夜各50%），同步4个相机（RGB、深度、语义分割）、LiDAR+语义LiDAR、前向雷达。雾效通过Koschmieder模型（相机通道）和Beer-Lambert定律（LiDAR通道）独立物理建模，设置三种校准能见度（160m、100m、50m）。每个场景有四种匹配变体，具备交叉校准的2D/3D边界框。通过语义分割质量审计验证8k图像中40m内车辆标注精度95.1%、召回率>99%。建立TransFusion、BEVFusion、YOLOv8-m的基线基准。

Result: 训练时混合多密度雾可收紧3D边界框几何质量，且无需额外数据扩展成本；在2D流程中，PSNR/SSIM等图像质量指标无法有效预测下游检测性能。

Conclusion: FogDrive将完全开源，包含数据生成框架，以加速鲁棒的多模态自动驾驶研究。

Abstract: Perception under adverse weather remains a critical bottleneck for reliable autonomous driving, yet existing benchmarks lack the systematic multi-modal alignments needed to evaluate robust sensor fusion. Real-world weather datasets suffer from uncontrolled collection and single-level, uncalibrated conditions, while synthetic alternatives either target camera-only restoration or lack the paired clean-and-foggy structure needed to benchmark "defog-then-detect" pipelines. We present FogDrive, a rigorously calibrated, multi-modal autonomous-driving dataset bridging data-centric engineering and robust machine learning. Built with the CARLA simulator, FogDrive contains 660 scenes (~133k fully annotated frames, 50:50 day/night) across four synchronized cameras (RGB, depth, semantic segmentation), a LiDAR and semantic-LiDAR pair, and front radar. Physically consistent fog is modeled independently on camera channels (Koschmieder model) and LiDAR channels (Beer-Lambert law) at three calibrated visibility densities (160m, 100m, 50m). Every scene ships in four matched variants (clean plus three graded fog levels) with cross-calibrated 2D and 3D bounding boxes. A semantic-segmentation-based quality audit over 8k images validates annotations at 95.1% precision and over 99% recall for vehicles within 40m. We establish baseline benchmarks with state-of-the-art architectures (TransFusion, BEVFusion, YOLOv8-m) across two paradigms: 3D multi-modal fusion and 2D image restoration. These yield critical data-centric insights: mixing multi-density fog during training tightens 3D bounding-box geometry without added data-scaling cost, while in 2D pipelines image-quality metrics (PSNR, SSIM) prove poor predictors of downstream detection performance. FogDrive will be fully open-sourced alongside our data-generation framework to accelerate robust, multi-modal research.

</details>


### [6] [MIME: Multimodal Interactive Motion Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22702)
*Addison Zucek,Prerit Gupta,Kamila Kuatova,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIME，首个专门为双人交互运动设计的多模态编码器，通过流式共注意力机制和课程对比学习，在文本-运动检索任务上相比基线提升12.8%的R@1，并能作为先验模块提升下游运动生成的语义对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本-运动表示学习大多针对单人运动，缺乏对双人交互中个体动作和角色间关系的联合建模能力，难以满足动画、AR/VR和具身AI等场景的需求。

Method: MIME采用基于流的共注意力机制（stream-based co-attention）来分别捕获个体和共享结构信息，引入显式交互特征（explicit interaction features），并利用课程式对比训练（curriculum-based contrastive training）进行多模态对齐。

Result: 在Inter-X数据集上，文本到运动检索R@1（2000样本库）相对提升12.8%；作为冻结的先验模块集成到TIMotion和InterMask中，在未见过的InterHuman数据集上提升了语义对齐指标，同时TIMotion的FID保持可比水平。

Conclusion: 交互感知的多模态编码能够有效改善多人运动检索性能，并且学到的表示可以跨数据集迁移，为下游运动生成任务提供语义先验支持。

Abstract: Text-motion representation learning has advanced rapidly, with growing interest in multi person interactions for animation, AR/VR, and embodied AI. These settings require representations that align language with both individual actor dynamics and the relationships between actors. We introduce the Multimodal Interactive Motion Encoder (MIME), which, to our knowledge, represents the first dedicated multimodal encoder designed specifically for two person interactive motion. MIME captures individual and shared structure using stream based co-attention with explicit interaction features and curriculum based contrastive training. On Inter-X text-motion retrieval, MIME consistently outperforms early and late fusion baselines across gallery sizes, achieving a 12.8% relative improvement in text-to-motion R@1 at a 2,000-sample gallery. We further evaluate MIME as a frozen auxiliary prior within TIMotion and InterMask on the unseen InterHuman dataset. MIME improves semantic alignment metrics while maintaining comparable FID in TIMotion. These results show that interaction aware multimodal encoding improves multi person motion retrieval and transfers across datasets to support downstream motion generation.

</details>


### [7] [Histopathological Spectrum-Guided Prostate Stratification via Segmentation-Assisted Diagnostic Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22703)
*Leyang Li,Lihua Chen,Huangang Hu,Tianhang Hao,Hao Cheng,Xin Zhang,Qianru Sun,Bingxu Lu,Wenlong Yu,Feng Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语言引导的分割辅助诊断Transformer模型(LSDT)，利用零样本分割提供解剖先验并进行多模态切片融合，用于前列腺癌mpMRI的四分类任务。在344名患者队列中取得0.633平均准确率和0.768联合召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前列腺癌mpMRI诊断依赖PI-RADS评估或二分类，存在主观性强、无法捕捉临床相关病理异质性的问题。同时现有数据集缺乏易与前列腺癌混淆的良性病变样本。

Method: 构建前列腺癌组织病理学谱数据集(PCa-HSD)定义四分类任务；提出LSDT模型，利用零样本分割提供解剖结构先验，执行有效的多模态切片融合以进行分类。

Result: 在344名患者队列的五折交叉验证中，平均准确率0.633，联合召回率0.768，方法在不同骨干网络上均有一致性提升。

Conclusion: 整合病理监督和解剖先验能显著增强细粒度前列腺MRI分类，为风险分层提供更具临床相关性的范式。

Abstract: Prostate cancer diagnosis with multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) is commonly based on PI-RADS assessment or binary classification, which suffer from subjectivity and fail to capture clinically relevant pathological heterogeneity. To address this limitation, we construct a Prostate Cancer Histopathology Spectrum Dataset (PCa-HSD) and formulate a clinically meaningful four-class classification task, addressing the underrepresentation of benign lesions that are easily confounded with prostate cancer in existing datasets. We propose Language-guided Segmentation-assisted Diagnostic Transformer model (LSDT), which leverages zero-shot segmentation to provide anatomical priors and performs effective multi-modal slice fusion for classification. Our proposed method consistently improves accuracy across backbones, achieving the best average accuracy of 0.633 and JointRecall of 0.768 in five-fold cross-validation on a cohort of 344 patients. These results demonstrate that integrating pathology supervision and anatomical priors significantly enhances fine-grained prostate MRI classification and provides a more clinically relevant paradigm for risk stratification. Code will be made publicly available in a future revision.

</details>


### [8] [Visible-Light Imaging Diagnosis of Neutral Particle Emission Tomography in the Tokamak Divertor: An Efficient Transformer-based Surrogate Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22704)
*Xiao Wang,Hao Si,Qiang Chen,Yu-Xiang Zhang,Beihe Zhang,Jianhua Yang,Qingquan Yang,Dengdi Sun,Wanli Lyu,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Delta-InvFormer，一种基于差分Transformer的骨干网络，通过处理连续视频帧捕捉等离子体动态，利用时空差分自注意力抑制噪声，实现光强二维空间分布的快速准确预测，在EAST设施数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核聚变研究中，利用可见光相机观测等离子体并预测其二维光强分布对后续科学实验至关重要，传统方法速度慢，亟需高效的深度学习方法加速这一过程。

Method: 提出Delta-InvFormer，核心为差分Transformer，以连续视频帧为输入，通过空间和时间差分自注意力机制抑制噪声干扰，提取高质量特征后融合为紧凑表示，再经解码器预测二维光强分布。

Result: 基于EAST装置真实实验数据，Delta-InvFormer显著加速了传统分布预测方法，同时保持了具有竞争力的重建精度。

Conclusion: Delta-InvFormer能高效预测等离子体二维光强分布，为核聚变实验中深度学习辅助的等离子体诊断提供了可行方案，且代码将开源。

Abstract: Nuclear fusion has made significant progress in recent years and is expected to become one of the most important pathways to addressing global energy challenges. This paper focuses on observing plasma using visible-light cameras, analyzing its spatio-temporal motion cues, and predicting the two-dimensional spatial distribution of light intensity, aiming to provide a foundational basis for future scientific experiments using deep neural networks. Specifically, we propose Delta-InvFormer, a novel backbone network centered on a differential Transformer. The key insight is that by taking consecutive video frames as input, we can better capture the dynamics of the plasma. Moreover, spatial and temporal differential self-attention effectively mitigates interference from noisy signals, ensuring high-quality feature extraction. These features are then fused into a compact and informative representation, which is fed into a decoder network to predict the distribution. Based on real experimental data collected from the Experimental Advanced Superconducting Tokamak (EAST) large-scale scientific facility, our results demonstrate that the proposed model not only significantly accelerates traditional methods for distribution prediction but also achieves competitive reconstruction accuracy. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenFusion

</details>


### [9] [EditCLEVR: A Paired-Scene Intervention Benchmark for Compositional Faithfulness of Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22705)
*Anuraag Gadehothur Karnam,Tarunesh Sathish*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditCLEVR是一个成对场景干预基准测试，通过在CLEVR场景中执行受控单属性编辑来评估以对象为中心的表示是否在语义上保持正确。提出了SGIA和Delta-SGIA指标，用于衡量干预后场景预测的语义忠实度。实验表明，在ground-truth掩码下仍存在OOD退化问题，且单独局部性或稳定性指标会高估语义忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以对象为中心的学习评估方法（如分割、单图像因子预测或下游准确率）无法直接检验在受控语义编辑下，每个对象的表示是否做出正确的语义变化。需要一种直接测试表示对场景干预的语义忠实度的基准。

Method: 构建EditCLEVR成对场景基准，包含before/after CLEVR渲染对，每对中要么对一个已知对象的一个已知属性进行精确编辑，要么进行无编辑重渲染以测量漂移。提出无探针的诊断指标（表示变化定位与稳定性）和基于探针解码的语义忠实度指标（SGIA和Delta-SGIA），分别要求完整后场景预测正确，或仅检查单一编辑模式的正确性。

Result: 在ground-truth掩码、学习型slot模型、SAM 2 + frozen-ViT模型和掩码-特征混合模型上的基线评估表明：CoGenT-OOD-core退化在ground-truth实例掩码下依然存在；掩码来源部分但不是全部解释了原生性能；仅靠局部性或稳定性指标会高估语义忠实度。

Conclusion: EditCLEVR提供了更严格的语义编辑测试，揭示了现有方法在OOD泛化和语义忠实度方面的不足，特别是局部性和稳定性指标会掩盖语义不忠实的缺陷。代码已开源。

Abstract: Object-centric learning aims to represent scenes as objects whose properties can be reused in new combinations. Existing evaluations usually score segmentation, single-image factor prediction, or downstream accuracy, but these tests do not directly ask whether a per-object representation behaves correctly under a controlled semantic edit. We introduce EditCLEVR, a paired-scene intervention benchmark in which each example contains a before/after pair of CLEVR-style renders with the same object indices and scene layout, and either exactly one known attribute change on one known object or a no-edit re-render for drift measurement. The protocol includes probe-free diagnostics for representation-change localization and stability, together with probe-decoded semantic faithfulness metrics that test whether the predicted scene change matches the intended intervention across in-distribution and compositional out-of-distribution (OOD) suites, allowing code-space movement and decoded object-attribute correctness to be evaluated separately. We introduce the semantic metric Scene-Graph Intervention Accuracy (SGIA), which requires the full after-scene prediction to be correct and the only predicted before-to-after semantic change to be the intended object-factor edit. We also establish Delta-SGIA as a companion diagnostic that checks the single-site change pattern without requiring the full after-scene graph to be correct. Baseline evaluations on ground-truth-mask backbones, learned-slot models, SAM 2 + frozen-ViT models, and one mask-feature hybrid indicate that CoGenT-OOD-core degradation can persist under ground-truth instance masks, that mask source accounts for part but not all of native performance, and that locality or stability alone can overstate semantic faithfulness. Code is available at https://github.com/torux-bughunter/EditCLEVR.

</details>


### [10] [LowAux-RDNet: Low-Pass Residual Supervision with Scene-Balanced Real-World Training for Single-Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22707)
*Jizhong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LowAux辅助目标（训练时对预测和真值进行低通滤波，提供低频约束）和场景平衡真实数据对，在多数据集统一评估基准上取得最高平均PSNR/SSIM/NCC和最低LMSE，但强语义反射仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单幅图像反射去除方法在跨数据集泛化上表现不均，评价标准因模型特定的resize、padding、量化等操作导致不一致，缺乏统一的公平基准。

Method: 1) 基于RDNet的显式分解流水线；2) LowAux训练辅助目标：对预测和真值进行对称低通滤波，提供稳定的低频约束，主监督仍为原始残差；3) 引入RRW场景平衡真实数据对增强跨数据集泛化；4) 建立统一公共基准（CEILNet, Real20, Postcard, Objects, Wild），统一评估器消除度量差异。

Result: 五个数据集宏观平均：PSNR 27.546 dB, SSIM 0.9220, NCC 0.9751, LMSE 0.004760。在对比的公开checkpoint和内部变体中取得最高宏观平均PSNR、SSIM、NCC和最低LMSE。

Conclusion: 主要收益是在不同反射分布上取得更均衡的性能，但Postcard数据集中清晰的语义反射仍具有挑战性。

Abstract: Single-image reflection removal aims to recover a clean transmission layer from one image captured through glass. We study an explicit decomposition pipeline built on RDNet and introduce LowAux, a training-only low-pass reflection auxiliary objective. The original residual target remains the main reflection supervision, while symmetrically filtered prediction and target provide a stable low-frequency constraint. We further incorporate scene-balanced real pairs from RRW to broaden real-scene coverage and improve cross-dataset generalization. To avoid evaluation discrepancies caused by model-specific resizing, padding, output quantization, and metric code, we build a unified public benchmark over CEILNet, Real20, Postcard, Objects, and Wild. Under the same evaluator, the proposed system obtains a five-dataset macro average of 27.546 dB PSNR, 0.9220 SSIM, 0.9751 NCC, and 0.004760 LMSE, achieving the highest macro-average PSNR, SSIM, and NCC and the lowest LMSE among the compared public checkpoints and internal variants. Per-dataset and qualitative analyses show that the main benefit is a more balanced performance across diverse reflection distributions, while clear semantic reflections in Postcard remain challenging.

</details>


### [11] [StepX-Edge: An On-Device UI Vision-Language Model via Architecture-Training-Deployment Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22708)
*Yin Wang,Haotian Hu,Jineng Han,Wentao Qiu,Zhenhua Ge,Liujian Tang,Fanyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StepX-Edge 是一个 0.9B 参数量的端侧 UI 视觉语言模型，通过架构、训练和部署三层协同设计，在移动设备上实现了 OCR、屏幕理解、VQA 和元素定位四种能力的最优平衡。其采用 ULVE 编码器、PDP 连接器和五阶段 StepX-Curriculum 训练框架，经 W4A16+KV8 量化后在骁龙 8 Gen5 上以 0.84 秒 TTFT、98 tok/s 解码和 1.4 GB 峰值内存稳定运行，在多个基准上超越 2B 级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧部署具有完整 UI 理解能力的视觉语言模型长期面临精度与效率的两难困境：一方面需要高精度的 OCR、屏幕理解、VQA 和元素定位，另一方面受限于移动芯片严格的计算、内存和功耗预算。现有工作要么牺牲一方换取另一方，要么仅停留在仿真阶段而缺乏真实设备验证。

Method: 提出三层协同设计：1) 架构层：UI 感知分层视觉编码 (ULVE) 和渐进维度投影 (PDP) 连接器处理屏幕的极端宽高比和细粒度感知，标准全注意力机制保证与移动 NPU 算子原生兼容；2) 训练层：五阶段 StepX-Curriculum 框架基于 UI 子任务间相互促进的观察，使四种能力在参数量受限下协同增长而非相互干扰；3) 部署层：模块级差异化的两阶段 PTQ-to-QAT 量化方案，将量化后精度损失控制在 1% 以内。

Result: StepX-Edge 在 <=1B 模型中取得最强综合 UI 理解能力，超越所有 2B-2.3B 基线模型：ScreenQA 上 88.76 F1，中文 OCRBench v2 上 57.25；在 RefCOCO 上达 92.0%，OCRBench v1 上 831，匹配 1.3B-2.3B 通用 VLM。经 W4A16+KV8 量化后，在骁龙 8 Gen5 上稳定运行，TTFT 约 0.84 秒，解码速度 98 tok/s，峰值内存 1.4 GB。

Conclusion: 通过三层协同设计，StepX-Edge 在极小参数量下实现了端侧全栈 UI 理解能力，在精度和效率上均超越了更大规模的模型。研究团队将开源训练数据、完整训练配方和量化部署流水线。

Abstract: Deploying a vision-language model with full UI understanding on end devices has long been trapped between accuracy and efficiency: on one side is the accuracy bar for OCR, screen understanding, visual question answering, and element grounding; on the other is the strict compute, memory, and power budget of mobile chips. Existing work either trades one for the other, or stops at simulation without real-device validation. We present StepX-Edge, a 0.9B-parameter on-device UI vision-language model that resolves this tension through three-layer co-design of architecture, training, and deployment. Architecturally, UI-aware Layered Visual Encoding (ULVE) and a Progressive Dimensionality Projection (PDP) connector target the extreme aspect ratios and fine-grained perception of screens, while standard full attention throughout ensures native compatibility with mainstream mobile NPU operators. For training, the five-stage StepX-Curriculum framework is designed around our observation of mutual-promotion effects among UI subtasks, so that all four capabilities grow synergistically under a tight parameter budget rather than interfering. For deployment, a module-wise differentiated two-stage PTQ-to-QAT quantization scheme keeps the post-quantization accuracy loss within 1%. StepX-Edge achieves the strongest overall UI understanding among <=1B models, surpassing all 2B-2.3B baselines on ScreenQA (88.76 F1) and Chinese OCRBench v2 (57.25), and matching 1.3B-2.3B general VLMs on RefCOCO (92.0%) and OCRBench v1 (831) with far fewer parameters. After W4A16+KV8 quantization, the model runs stably on Snapdragon 8 Gen5 devices with ~0.84 s TTFT, 98 tok/s decode, and 1.4 GB peak memory. We will open-source the training data, the full training recipe, and the quantization deployment pipeline.

</details>


### [12] [RMS@CC-MMD 2026: Multimodal Misogyny Detection via Geometric Interaction and Multi-View Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22709)
*Md. Ajwad Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMVC（几何交互与多视图共识）方法，用于多模态网络迷因中的厌女内容检测。该方法通过几何交互层（Hadamard积+余弦相似度）建模视觉-文本跨模态对齐，并采用多视图共识策略（原始文本、长度过滤文本、英文翻译文本）缓解OCR噪声和代码混合转写带来的分布偏移。在ICMI 2026 CC-MMD挑战赛Task A中，该系统在马拉雅拉姆语分区获得第2名（Macro F1: 0.892），中文分区第3名（Macro F1: 0.895），泰米尔语分区第5名（Macro F1: 0.521）。错误分析揭示了在德拉维达语系和中文文化背景下建模本地化转写和代码混合讽刺的开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网迷因的泛滥给自动化内容审核带来新挑战，特别是在厌女内容检测方面。迷因常依赖视觉与文本模态间的语义冲突，其仇恨意图隐晦且具有文化根基。现有静态特征拼接方法无法有效处理跨模态对齐问题。

Method: 提出GeoMVC方法，包含：(1) 几何交互层（Geometric Interaction Layer），利用冻结的视觉和文本嵌入之间的Hadamard积和余弦相似度建模跨模态对齐；(2) 多视图共识策略（Multi-View Consensus），聚合原始文本、长度过滤文本和英文翻译文本三个视图的预测结果，以缓解由噪声OCR和代码混合转写引起的分布偏移。

Result: 在ICMI 2026 CC-MMD挑战赛Task A中：马拉雅拉姆语分区排名第2（Macro F1: 0.892），中文分区排名第3（Macro F1: 0.895），泰米尔语分区排名第5（Macro F1: 0.521）。

Conclusion: 详细的错误分析揭示了在德拉维达语系和中文文化背景下，建模本地化转写和代码混合讽刺仍然是开放性挑战。

Abstract: The proliferation of internet memes has introduced new complexities to automated content moderation, particularly in detecting misogyny. Memes often rely on a semantic clash between visual and textual modalities, where hateful intent is implicit and culturally grounded. This paper presents GeoMVC (Geometric Interaction and Multi-View Consensus), developed for the CC-MMD Grand Challenge at ICMI 2026. To address the limitations of static feature concatenation, a Geometric Interaction Layer is proposed that models cross-modal alignment via Hadamard products and cosine similarity between frozen visual and textual embeddings. We further mitigate distribution shifts caused by noisy OCR and code-mixed transliteration through a Multi-View Consensus strategy, aggregating predictions across raw, length-filtered, and English-translated text views. The system achieved Rank 2 in the Malayalam partition (Macro F1: 0.892) and Rank 3 in the Chinese partition (Macro F1: 0.895) on Task A, while securing Rank 5 in the Tamil partition (Macro F1: 0.521). A detailed error analysis on the development partition highlights open challenges in modeling localized transliteration and code-mixed sarcasm across Dravidian and Chinese cultural contexts.

</details>


### [13] [scMIR: a vision-language foundation model for single-cell light microscopy image representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22712)
*Yifan Shang,Jiahui Tan,Xiangxiang Zeng,Renjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: scMIR是一个面向单细胞光学显微镜图像的视觉-语言基础模型，通过自监督图像重建与文本引导跨模态对齐，在统一表示空间中编码形态学和生物学语义信息。该模型在207,957个图像-文本对上预训练，覆盖多种细胞类型、显微镜模态和扰动条件，在16个基准数据集的细胞分类、聚类、表型推断和批次效应校正等任务上优于现有通用模型和任务导向方法，展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞显微镜图像表示学习方法存在两个局限：一是任务导向方法受限于特定数据集和预定义任务，难以跨细胞类型、显微镜模态和实验条件泛化；二是通用方法对实验背景和生物学上下文信息的利用有限，在复杂表型分析中面临挑战。

Method: 提出scMIR视觉-语言基础模型，采用自监督图像重建与文本引导跨模态对齐的协同训练策略，将形态学特征与生物学语义信息编码至统一表示空间。在207,957个图像-文本对上预训练，数据涵盖多种细胞类型、显微镜模态和扰动条件。

Result: 在16个基准数据集上系统评估，scMIR在细胞分类、聚类、表型推断和批次效应校正等复杂任务中均优于现有通用模型和任务导向方法，且无需任务特定微调即可展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: scMIR有望通过支持多种下游分析任务，推动高通量表型分析工作流的标准化和自动化。

Abstract: Single-cell light microscopy images have become an important data source for characterizing cell phenotypes, but their complexity and heterogeneity pose challenges to high-throughput automated analysis. Existing representation learning methods mostly rely on task-oriented modeling, which is limited by specific datasets and predefined tasks, making them difficult to generalize across different cell types and microscopy modalities, and experimental conditions. Although general-purpose methods have improved the generalization ability of image representation in recent years, their limited utilization of experimental background and biological context information still poses challenges in complex phenotypic analysis. Here, we propose scMIR, a vision-language foundation model for single-cell light microscopy image representation. By synergistically combining self-supervised image reconstruction with text-guided cross-modal alignment, scMIR can simultaneously encode morphological and biological semantic information in a unified representation space. scMIR is pre-trained on 207,957 image-text pairs, covering various cell types, microscopy modalities, and perturbation conditions. scMIR outperforms existing general models and task-oriented methods as systematically evaluated on various complex tasks using 16 benchmark datasets, including cell classification, clustering, phenotype inference, and batch effect correction tasks. Furthermore, scMIR shows a strong generalization ability across various tasks without requiring task-specific fine-tuning. With its unique advantages, we envision scMIR may promote the standardization and automation of high-throughput phenotyping workflows through supporting various downstream analysis tasks.

</details>


### [14] [Real-Time Semantic Segmentation with Optimized RetinaNet Architectures for Embedded Automotive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22714)
*Sai Sidharth D*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Opt-RetinaSeg，一种基于RetinaNet衍生的轻量化语义分割架构，通过混合轻量骨干网络、重构FPN、焦点损失引导的分割头以及通道剪枝、INT8量化、知识蒸馏三阶段优化，在Jetson Xavier NX和QCS610平台上实现73.9% mIoU和70.4 FPS，较ResNet-50基线加速7.4倍、模型缩小4倍，精度损失不足3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式汽车平台对计算、内存和功耗有严格限制，而实时感知是ADAS和自动驾驶的基础需求。现有的语义分割方法难以在资源受限的嵌入式硬件上同时满足高精度和实时性要求。

Method: 基于RetinaNet检测框架改造为密集像素预测架构，具体包括：(1) 用混合轻量特征提取器替换标准ResNet-50骨干网络；(2) 重构FPN以减少冗余多尺度计算；(3) 引入由焦点损失启发的不平衡类别校准的紧凑分割头；(4) 三阶段优化流水线：结构化通道剪枝、训练后INT8量化、知识蒸馏。

Result: 在Cityscapes和BDD100K数据集上，部署于NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX和Qualcomm QCS610 SoC，达到73.9% mIoU和70.4 FPS，相比ResNet-50基线推理速度提升7.4倍，模型尺寸减小4倍，精度损失小于3%。

Conclusion: 经过系统优化的RetinaNet衍生架构能够成为嵌入式汽车感知管线中实时语义分割的可行方案。

Abstract: Real-time perception is a foundational requirement for advanced driver assistance systems (ADAS) and autonomous vehicles, yet embedded automotive platforms impose severe constraints on compute, memory, and power. This paper presents an optimized semantic segmentation architecture derived from the RetinaNet detection framework, adapted for dense pixel-wise prediction and tailored for deployment on resource-constrained embedded hardware. The proposed architecture, termed Opt-RetinaSeg, replaces the standard ResNet-50 backbone with a hybrid lightweight feature extractor, restructures the Feature Pyramid Network (FPN) to reduce redundant multi-scale computation, and introduces a compact segmentation head guided by focal-loss-inspired class balancing to address the severe foreground-background imbalance common in road scenes. We further apply a three-stage optimization pipeline consisting of structured channel pruning, post-training INT8 quantization, and knowledge distillation from a high-capacity teacher network. Evaluated on the Cityscapes and BDD100K datasets and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX and a Qualcomm QCS610 automotive SoC, the proposed model achieves 73.9% mIoU at 70.4 FPS, representing a 7.4x inference speedup and a 4x reduction in model size relative to the ResNet-50 baseline, with less than 3% accuracy degradation. These results indicate that RetinaNet-derived architectures, when systematically optimized, are viable candidates for real-time semantic segmentation in embedded automotive perception pipelines

</details>


### [15] [Visual Token Compression Enhances Robustness of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22716)
*Shishen Gu,Jiequan Cui,Wenbo Hu,Zenglin Shi,Zhenzhen Hu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次证明视觉token剪枝可以增强多模态大语言模型的鲁棒性，通过移除与语言特征空间存在分布偏移的视觉token，有效缓解越狱攻击和幻觉问题，同时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉与语言模态无法完美对齐，不对齐的视觉token可能作为分布外输入，导致不可预测的输出并引入安全漏洞（如越狱攻击和幻觉）。因此，需要一种方法来减少这些有问题的视觉token，从而增强模型鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 测量每个视觉token与语言特征空间的距离；2) 将距离较大的视觉token识别为分布外(OOD)token；3) 在鲁棒剪枝层中迭代剪除这些OOD视觉token。

Result: 在7个多样化基准测试中，防御越狱攻击平均提升13.29%，在缓解幻觉方面取得有竞争力的性能，同时在MME等通用数据集上保持强结果。

Conclusion: 视觉token剪枝不仅能提升多模态大语言模型的安全性（防御越狱攻击和幻觉），还能作为副产品降低推理成本，是一种高效且实用的鲁棒性增强方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we show for the first time that visual token pruning enhances the robustness of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), mitigating vulnerabilities such as jailbreak attacks and hallucinations. Given that vision and language modalities cannot be perfectly aligned, the misaligned visual tokens might act as out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs, leading to unpredictable outputs and introducing potential vulnerabilities. Building on this insight, we aim to enhance model robustness against jailbreaks and hallucinations by reducing OOD visual tokens at robust-pruning layers, while also reducing inference cost as a side benefit. Specifically, we measure the distance between each visual token and the language feature space. Then, visual tokens with large distances are identified as OOD tokens, which can be iteratively pruned. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we evaluate it on seven diverse popular benchmarks. Notably, our method yields an average improvement of 13.29\% in defending jailbreak attacks, consistently achieves competitive performance in mitigating hallucinations, and maintains strong results on general datasets like MME.

</details>


### [16] [TOM-GS: Editable Video Representation via Temporal Opacity Modulation of Static 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22717)
*Marek Lisowski,Łukasz Smoliński,Kornel Howil,Piotr Biliński,Marcin Mazur,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TOM-GS提出了一种基于常规3D高斯与连续时间不透明度公式的可编辑视频表示方法，通过为每个高斯的不透明度分配可学习的时间均值和尺度，实现静态空间几何下的平滑时序淡入淡出，在保持视觉保真度的同时支持多种编辑操作，并兼容现有3D编辑工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示和动态3D高斯溅射方法虽在视频处理上表现优异，但难以生成易于编辑的表示；近期方法引入复杂空间变形或折叠分布，限制了优化灵活性和下游编辑能力。

Method: 摒弃复杂变形，采用常规3D高斯并配备连续时间不透明度公式；为每个高斯的不透明度分配可学习的时间均值和尺度，使静态3D空间分量能够平滑淡入淡出；利用鲁棒的现成姿态估计保持静态空间几何。

Result: 在视觉保真度上优于先前的可编辑视频表示方法，且与现有3D编辑工具无缝兼容。

Conclusion: TOM-GS通过简洁的时序不透明度机制在保持静态空间几何的同时实现了高效的可编辑视频表示，兼具高视觉质量和编辑灵活性。

Abstract: While Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) and dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieve impressive results in video processing, they often fall short of producing representations that are easily editable. Recent methods address this by introducing complex spatial deformations or folded distributions, which constrain optimization and reduce flexibility for downstream editing. In this paper, we introduce TOM-GS, an editable video representation that forgoes complex deformations in favor of regular 3D Gaussians equipped with a continuous temporal opacity formulation. By assigning a learnable temporal mean and scale to the opacity of each Gaussian, our model enables static 3D spatial components to fade smoothly in and out of the scene. Grounded by robust, off-the-shelf pose estimation, our approach maintains a static spatial geometry that naturally supports a wide range of manual and physics-based edits. TOM-GS outperforms prior editable video representations in visual fidelity, while its reliance on standard 3D Gaussians ensures seamless compatibility with established 3D editing tools.

</details>


### [17] [DAMamba-UNet3D: A Parameter-Efficient Mamba State Space U-Net with Dynamic Adaptive Scan for 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22718)
*Mohammad Arafat Hussain,Ellen Grant,Yangming Ou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数高效的基于状态空间模型（SSM）的U-Net架构DAMamba-UNet3D，用于3D医学图像分割。该模型在编码器阶段E2-E4引入学习数据依赖重排序的三平面3D-DAS块，其余部分保持卷积。在BraTS 2020上，~5.3M参数的模型以比SegMamba（~70M）低13倍的参数量达到相近性能；大尺度版本DAMamba-L（~70M）超越SegMamba。消融实验表明仅编码器放置DAS是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学SSM分割器依赖固定扫描模式和大参数预算，且动态自适应扫描（DAS）尚未应用于医学影像和3D体积。作者希望设计参数高效的SSM-based U-Net，在保持线性复杂度同时引入数据依赖的全局上下文建模能力。

Method: 提出DAMamba-UNet3D混合编码器-解码器架构：在编码器阶段E2-E4集成三平面3D-DAS（动态自适应扫描）块，其余阶段（E1、E5、瓶颈、解码器）保留卷积。DAS通过学习数据依赖重排序后再进行选择性扫描。同时提出DAMamba-L大尺度变体，仅编码器使用DAMamba，瓶颈使用卷积。

Result: BraTS 2020五折交叉验证：DAMamba-UNet3D（~5.3M参数）平均Dice 0.815±0.013，以约13倍更少参数接近SegMamba（0.824±0.014，~70M）。DAMamba-L（~70M）达到0.829±0.012，超越重训练的SegMamba 0.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 学习的三平面DAS在混合U-Net中与SegMamba的固定三方向Mamba（ToM）扫描具有竞争力，且在大尺度设计下可能更优。编码器仅放置DAS是关键设计选择，瓶颈和解码器的SSM块会降低性能。

Abstract: We propose parameter-efficient SSM-based U-Net architectures for 3D medical image segmentation. Convolutional U-Nets afford O(n) local mixing per layer but lack explicit global context; transformers provide global reasoning at O(n^2) cost in sequence length $n$. State-space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, offer $O(n)$ global propagation per block. Yet, existing medical SSM segmenters rely on fixed scan patterns and large parameter budgets. Dynamic Adaptive Scan (DAS), which learns data-dependent reordering before selective scan, has not been applied to medical imaging or extended to 3D volumes. We propose DAMamba-UNet3D, a hybrid encoder-decoder that integrates tri-plane 3D-DAS blocks at encoder stages E2-E4 while retaining convolutions elsewhere (~5.3M parameters). On BraTS 2020 five-fold cross-validation, DAMamba-UNet3D achieves mean Dice 0.815+/-0.013 (full-volume per-case evaluation) at ~13x lower parameter cost than SegMamba (0.824+\-0.014, ~70M). At comparable scale, DAMamba-L (~70M), a wide DAS-native variant with encoder-only DAMamba and a convolutional bottleneck, reaches 0.829+\-0.012, surpassing retrained SegMamba by 0.5pt. Component ablations show that encoder-only DAS placement is critical as bottleneck and decoder SSM blocks lower Dice. Together, the results suggest that learned tri-plane DAS in a hybrid U-Net is competitive with, and under our large-scale design may improve upon, SegMamba's fixed Tri-orientated Mamba (ToM) scanning on BraTS 2020. Code: https://github.com/marafathussain/DAMamba-UNet3D.

</details>


### [18] [$γ$-Bridge: A Look-Parametric Diffusion Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22719)
*Xuran Hu,Yujie Zhu,Tengxi Wang,Jilong Li,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出γ-Bridge，一种基于伽马噪声物理模型（视数L）的参数化桥接扩散方法，可在仅用合成数据训练的条件下，零样本泛化到真实SAR图像去斑，并在多个星载/机载传感器数据上取得领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散去噪器使用抽象信噪比调度而非物理视数L建模前向过程，导致不同部署场景需分别训练模型，且从合成伽马噪声到真实SAR图像的迁移学习缺乏干净参考图像，难以泛化。

Method: 提出γ-Bridge，其桥接调度L(t)通过精确乘性伽马边缘分布连接含噪观测(L_obs)与干净极限；推导出闭式伽马-莱维反向后验，支持随机和确定性过程；引入观测条件化和两步一致性损失稳定低信噪比单视推理；训练仅在L_obs=1的合成数据上，实现全视数网格零样本恢复；结合同质斑块视数估计器处理多传感器数据。

Result: 在六个星载和机载SAR传感器数据上无需传感器特定微调，在标准合成基准上取得领先结果。

Conclusion: γ-Bridge提供了物理可解释的输入和输出控制，克服了现有去噪器在跨传感器泛化上的局限，实现了零样本真实SAR去斑。

Abstract: Multiplicative Gamma noise is a signal-dependent degradation in coherent imaging; synthetic aperture radar (SAR) despeckling is its most prominent real-world instance. Existing diffusion denoisers parameterize their forward process by abstract signal-to-noise schedules rather than by the physical look number $L$, so different deployment scenarios typically require separately trained models, and transfer from synthetic Gamma training to real SAR remains challenging without clean ground truth. We introduce $γ$-Bridge, a look-parametric bridge whose schedule $L(t)$ connects the noisy observation at $L_{obs}$ to the clean limit through exact multiplicative Gamma marginals. Its closed-form Gamma--Lévy reverse posterior admits both stochastic and deterministic processes, while observation conditioning and a two-step consistency loss stabilize multi-step inference in the low-SNR single-look regime. Because bridge time directly represents $L$, one conditioned network can smart-start from any admissible input look and stop at a target look number. These two orthogonal controls enable zero-shot restoration over the full admissible grid after training only at $L_{obs} = 1$ on natural images with synthetic Gamma corruption. Combined with a homogeneous-patch look estimator, $γ$-Bridge processes data from six spaceborne and airborne SAR sensors without sensor-specific fine-tuning, achieving leading results on standard synthetic benchmarks while providing physically interpretable input and output controls absent from prior denoisers. Codes are released \href{https://github.com/Teriri1999/GammaBridge}{here}.

</details>


### [19] [An Interactive Vision Language Platform for Cognitive Remediation in Schizophrenia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22721)
*Nassira Ait Mehdi,Milissa Temmam,Slimane Larabi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉语言模型的自动化框架，用于精神分裂症患者认知康复任务中的动作验证。系统通过摄像头监控桌面场景（含微缩道路、环岛、公园及玩具车），分析患者执行音频指令时的操作视频，利用微调后的视觉语言模型生成语义描述并与指令比对，实现客观、可扩展的自动化评估。在4634个视频样本上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知康复任务中，患者的结构化动作评估依赖临床医生手动观察，存在主观性强、可扩展性差的问题。为精神分裂症患者提供客观、可规模化的自动化动作验证方法，以减少临床人力负担并提高评估一致性。

Method: 构建摄像头监控的桌面环境（微缩道路、环岛、公园、玩具车），患者根据音频指令操作玩具车完成空间动作；通过视频追踪手部和玩具运动，使用微调后的视觉语言模型生成视频语义描述，并与初始文本指令进行高层级比对验证。收集4634个桌面认知康复视频场景用于评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架能够有效桥接低层物理遥测与高层临床反馈，在认知康复动作验证任务上实现了客观、可扩展的方案。

Conclusion: 该自动化框架为精神分裂症患者的认知康复提供了可扩展的客观评估方案，减少了临床主观性，有望推动高级认知康复干预的规模化应用。

Abstract: Cognitive remediation tasks often require patients to perform structured actions involving object manipulation and sequential reasoning. For patients diagnosed with schizophrenia, these tasks are crucial for addressing severe cognitive deficits. However, evaluating the correctness of these physical actions generally relies on manual observation by clinicians, which introduces subjectivity and limits the scalability of therapeutic interventions. In this paper, we propose an automated framework based on Vision-Language Models for action verification in cognitive remediation tasks tailored for schizophrenia rehabilitation. The proposed system relies on a camera-monitored tabletop environment composed of structured miniature scenes including roads, a roundabout, a park, and toy vehicles. Patients receive audio instructions describing goal-oriented spatial actions to perform by manipulating a toy vehicle. These interactive physical activities are specifically designed to stimulate targeted cognitive functions, such as sustained attention, motor coordination, spatial navigation, and cognitive flexibility. To verify the correctness of the performed actions without requiring continuous clinical oversight, the system analyzes the video feed tracking the patient's hand and toy movements. A fine-tuned Vision-Language Model interprets the recorded video sequences and generates semantic descriptions of the observed activities, enabling high-level verification of the executed actions with respect to the initial textual instructions. A dedicated dataset of 4634 tabletop cognitive remediation video scenarios was collected to evaluate the proposed approach. Experimental results demonstrate that our specialized framework effectively bridges low-level physical telemetry with high-level clinical feedback, presenting a scalable and objective solution for advanced cognitive rehabilitation.

</details>


### [20] [A New Kind of Adversarial Example: Measuring the Human-Model Gap, and Its Relationship to OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22722)
*Ali Borji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种与常见对抗攻击截然相反的"清晰可见攻击"：添加人眼明显可见的大扰动，但模型仍保持正确预测，而人类已无法识别图像。实验在MNIST、CIFAR-10和ImageNet上进行，发现：人类识别率远低于模型；传统OOD检测和校准方法完全失效；现有防御手段（包括对抗训练）几乎无效。机制分析表明该攻击优先破坏低层纹理而非边缘/形状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作可大规模生成"清晰可见但模型仍正确预测"的对抗样本，但留下了三个未回答的问题：(1)人类是否真的比模型表现更差？(2)标准分布外检测和校准工具能否捕捉到这种样本？(3)现有防御手段能否缓解这种攻击？本文旨在系统回答这三个问题。

Method: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和ImageNet三个数据集上，通过独立识别器代理（CIFAR-10上约49%识别率，模型保持100%）、小规模人类实验（N=5）、CLIP零样本代理以及匹配幅度的高斯噪声对照实验来验证人类-模型性能差距；使用置信度/能量/马氏距离等检测器评估OOD检测能力；测试对抗训练等多种经典防御的有效性；进行机制分析探究攻击对纹理与边缘/形状结构的破坏模式。

Result: (i)人类识别率显著低于模型（CIFAR-10上代理识别器约49%，模型100%），小规模人类实验直接佐证，且该差距不能由信号损失解释（匹配幅度的高斯噪声更快破坏可识别性）；(ii)基于置信度和能量的OOD检测器及校准工具完全失效（检测率0%，ECE≈0），而特征空间的马氏距离检测器可100%检测，但自适应攻击者可零代价绕过；(iii)包括对抗训练（鲁棒准确率45%）在内的经典防御均无法降低攻击成功率（与大ε_l2抵抗的相关性r≈0）；机制分析表明攻击优先破坏低层纹理。

Conclusion: 这种"清晰可见但模型仍正确分类"的对抗样本揭示了人类与模型之间存在根本性的感知差异，且现有检测和防御机制均存在严重盲区。这挑战了当前对"对抗扰动必须不可察觉"的普遍认知，为对抗鲁棒性研究提出了新的维度和挑战。

Abstract: Almost all adversarial attacks add an imperceptible perturbation to fool a model. We instead study the opposite: a large, clearly visible perturbation that causes the model to keep its original, correct prediction, even though a human would no longer recognize the image. Prior work showed such examples can be generated at scale but left three questions untested: whether humans really perform worse than the model, whether standard out-of-distribution (OOD) detection and calibration tools catch it, and whether existing defenses mitigate it. We answer all three on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and ImageNet. (i) An independent recognizer proxy drops to ~49% on CIFAR-10 while the model stays at 100% -- a gap a small human pilot (N=5) corroborates directly and that is not explained by signal loss (a matched-magnitude Gaussian control degrades recognizability faster); a CLIP zero-shot proxy confirms the gap at ImageNet scale too. (ii) Confidence- and energy-based OOD detectors and calibration are structurally blind (0% detection, ECE ~= 0), while a feature-space Mahalanobis detector flags 100% -- but is evaded by an adaptive attacker at no cost to success. (iii) No classical defense, including adversarial training (45% robust accuracy), reduces attack success (correlation with large-epsilon_l resistance r ~= 0). A mechanistic analysis further shows the attack destroys low-level texture far faster than edge/shape structure.

</details>


### [21] [Visual Information Extraction from Documents via Classification-Guided Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22723)
*Huafu Li,Guo Chen,Jia Xia,Lei Wang,Wei Du,Yun Yao,Weijun Peng,Liming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分类引导的大视觉语言模型（LVLM）框架，通过解耦文档类型分类与内容提取，结合上下文学习（ICL）动态提示工程，实现多类型视觉信息抽取的零样本推理，在真实招投标数据集上以7B模型超越强监督基线18.35个百分点的F1-score（86.43% vs 68.08%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIE方法要么依赖顺序OCR流水线，要么需要大量标注数据和布局特定训练的端到端模型，可扩展性差，难以应对高布局多样性和真实场景中的印章、水印、低对比度等干扰。

Method: 提出分类引导的LVLM框架：（1）将文档类型分类与内容提取解耦；（2）基于ICL（上下文学习）的动态提示工程注入任务特定知识，实现跨布局的零样本推理；（3）可选领域特定微调进一步提升性能。理论视角上，该方法可视为一种条件计算形式，降低任务不确定性并提高提示推理的信息效率。

Result: 在包含16种证书类型的真实招投标数据集上，基于Qwen2.5-VL-7B的零样本方法F1-score为86.43%（超强监督基线68.08%），归一化编辑距离（NED）为0.90（超基线0.67）。可选微调后F1达93.65%，NED达0.93，对印章、水印、低对比度具有优异鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为办公自动化中的复杂文档理解提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案，代码和微调模型已开源。

Abstract: Visual information extraction (VIE) from visually rich documents remains challenging due to high layout variability and real-world impairments. Existing methods typically rely on sequential OCR pipelines or end-to-end models requiring extensive labeled data and layout-specific training, limiting their scalability.We propose a classification-guided large vision-language model (LVLM) framework for multi-type VIE that achieves high accuracy with minimal supervision. The approach decouples document-type classification from content extraction and employs in-context learning (ICL)-based dynamic prompt engineering to inject task-specific knowledge, enabling robust zero-shot inference across diverse layouts. From a theoretical perspective, the proposed method can be viewed as a form of conditional computation that reduces task uncertainty and improves information efficiency during prompt-based inference. Evaluated on a real-world bidding dataset with 16 certificate types, our zero-shot method (based on Qwen2.5-VL-7B) outperforms a strong supervised baseline by 18.35 percentage points in F1-score (86.43\% vs. 68.08\%) and 0.23 in normalized edit distance (0.90 vs. 0.67). Optional domain-specific fine-tuning further improves performance to 93.65\% F1 and 0.93 NED, demonstrating superior robustness against seals, watermarks, and low contrast. The framework offers an efficient, scalable solution for complex document understanding in office automation. Code is available at https://github.com/FairmeHIT/Multi-VIE, and fine-tuned models at https://huggingface.co/fairme/Qwen2.5-VL-7B-SFT.

</details>


### [22] [Structural Preservation Governs Data Augmentation in Deep Learning-Based Laser Speckle Material Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22725)
*Mohamed Abdallah Salem,Nourhan Zein Diab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据增强对激光散斑成像中基于散斑纹理的材料分类任务的影响，发现保持散斑空间结构（如空间相关噪声）的增强策略能提升模型鲁棒性，而破坏结构（如高斯模糊、独立像素噪声）会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准数据增强策略假设图像纹理是通用且独立的，但激光散斑图案具有相干干涉产生的结构化随机空间与频率统计特征，传统增强方法（如高斯模糊）会破坏这些关键结构，因此需要针对散斑成像的物理特性重新审视增强策略。

Method: 在SensiCut数据集上，使用ResNet18和EfficientNet-B0两种架构，在参数化增强框架（包括旋转、高斯模糊、独立高斯噪声、空间相关散斑感知噪声、强度抖动、空间掩蔽）下训练，采用macro F1-score评估，并分别对每种架构建立OLS回归模型分析增强参数与性能的关系。

Result: 高斯模糊对两类模型均有显著负效应（p<0.001），独立像素噪声同样有害（p=0.003和p=0.001），而空间相关扰动显著正向提升性能（p=0.004和p=0.001），回归模型解释力高（R²=0.796和0.879）。

Conclusion: 在激光散斑成像中，增强策略的有效性主要取决于是否保留散斑的空间组织结构，而非扰动幅度大小，这为相干光学传感领域设计物理感知的数据增强方法提供了依据。

Abstract: Data augmentation is routinely used to improve generalization in image classification, but the assumptions underlying standard policies are poorly matched to coherent imaging. Laser speckle patterns are not generic textures; they arise from coherent interference, and their discriminative content is carried by structured stochastic spatial and frequency statistics. This study examines how controlled augmentation perturbations influence speckle-based material classification on the SensiCut dataset. We train ResNet18 and EfficientNet-B0 under a parametric augmentation framework comprising rotation, Gaussian blur, independent Gaussian noise, spatially correlated speckle-aware noise, intensity jitter, and spatial masking, and evaluate test performance using macro F1-score averaged over three random seeds. Separate ordinary least squares models link augmentation parameters to performance for each architecture. Across both models, Gaussian blur exerts a strong negative effect (p < 0.001), indicating that low-pass filtering suppresses high-frequency structure that is informative for material discrimination. Independent pixel-wise noise is likewise harmful (p = 0.003 for EfficientNet-B0 and p = 0.001 for ResNet18), consistent with disruption of local spatial coherence. In contrast, spatially correlated perturbations yield significant positive coefficients (p = 0.004 for EfficientNet-B0 and p = 0.001 for ResNet18), showing that variability can improve robustness when it preserves speckle organization. The fitted models explain a substantial fraction of performance variation (R2 = 0.796 for EfficientNet-B0 and R2 = 0.879 for ResNet18). These results show that, in laser speckle imaging, augmentation effectiveness is determined primarily by structural preservation rather than perturbation magnitude. The findings motivate physics-aware augmentation design for coherent optical sensing.

</details>


### [23] [PCA: Persistence-Aware Compression and Aggregation for Fast Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22726)
*Zihan Song,Shuo Ye,Bo Zhao,Ruixin Zhang,Jiayu Zhang,Shouhong Ding,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的持久感知压缩与聚合方法（PCA），通过动态下采样和运动增强两个模块，在编码前高效去除视频冗余帧，实现1.8-2.5倍加速且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言模型在处理长视频时存在严重的帧冗余问题，导致推理效率低下，现有方法难以在保持高保真视觉信息的同时实现高效压缩。

Method: PCA方法包含两个模块：1）动态下采样（DD）模块，通过分析帧间相似性自适应去除冗余帧；2）持久感知运动增强（PAME）模块，通过聚合邻域帧的时间上下文来增强所选关键帧，确保信息完整性。

Result: 该方法在多个基准上一致优于现有方法，相比基线VLLM实现1.8倍至2.5倍的加速，同时提升模型性能。

Conclusion: PCA作为一种训练无关、可即插即用的方法，有效解决了长视频推理中的效率瓶颈，兼顾了计算效率与性能提升。

Abstract: Despite advances in Video Large Language Models (VLLMs) that have displayed promising outcomes in video understanding, the redundancy in the long-duration frames remains a hindrance to efficient reasoning. This paper introduces a training-free $\mathbf{P}$ersistence-Aware $\mathbf{C}$ompression and $\mathbf{A}$ggregation (PCA) method designed to preserve high-fidelity raw visual information before the encoding stage. PCA can be built on arbitrary VLLMs and consists of two modules: 1) A Dynamic Downsampling (DD) module that adaptively removes redundant frames by analyzing frame-wise similarity. 2) A Persistence-Aware Motion Enhancement (PAME) module that enriches each selected keyframe by aggregating the temporal context of its neighbors, ensuring that essential information is preserved even after aggressive frame reduction. Our approach substantially reduces the computation of long-context modeling, while enhancing the performance of the baseline model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PCA consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in both efficiency and accuracy, achieving a speedup of 1.8$\times$ to 2.5$\times$ compared to the baseline VLLM. The code is open-sourced at https://github.com/Heisenberg10110/PCA.

</details>


### [24] [Trustworthy Medical Segmentation: Uncertainty-Aware U-Net Evaluation Under Clinical Image Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22727)
*Pranav Kaliaperumal,Manisha Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的框架，用于评估医学图像分割模型在临床退化（传感器噪声、运动伪影、低分辨率、对比度变化等）下的不确定性。使用合成多模态脑肿瘤MRI数据、U-Net/Attention U-Net加蒙特卡洛Dropout，评估了8种退化类型、5个严重等级下的分割精度、校准、故障检测和选择性预测覆盖。干净数据上全肿瘤Dice达0.990，严重高斯噪声下骤降至0.089；预测不确定性与分割误差相关（r=0.53），故障检测AUROC达0.843。结果表明不确定性推理可作为放射学工作流中的实用安全层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割模型在理想条件下报告高精度，但在临床退化（噪声、运动、低分辨率、对比度变化等）下可能无声失败，缺乏可靠预警机制。需要系统评估不确定性感知方法在这些退化下的鲁棒性和故障检测能力。

Method: 使用生物物理体模模拟器生成遵循BraTS协议的多模态脑肿瘤MRI合成数据；训练U-Net和Attention U-Net基线模型进行多类肿瘤子区分割，并用蒙特卡洛Dropout增强以估计逐体素不确定性；在8种临床相关退化类型、5个严重等级下评估分割精度、校准、故障检测和选择性预测覆盖。

Result: 干净数据上Attention U-Net全肿瘤Dice为0.990；严重高斯噪声下降至0.089。预测不确定性与分割误差相关（Pearson r=0.53），故障检测AUROC达0.843。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知推理可作为医生在环放射学工作流中的实用安全层，并开源代码、模型和评估协议以支持直接复现。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models often report high benchmark accuracy under ideal imaging conditions, yet their failures under clinical degradation can be quiet: sensor noise, patient motion, low- resolution acquisition, and contrast variability may all alter model behavior without producing an obvious warning. We present a reproducible framework for evaluating uncertainty-aware segmentation under con- trolled clinical degradation. Our experiments use a synthetic multimodal brain tumor MRI cohort generated with a biophysical phantom simulator that follows the BraTS protocol. We train U-Net and Attention U-Net baselines for multi-class tumor sub-region segmentation and augment both models with Monte Carlo dropout to estimate per-voxel uncertainty. Across eight clinically motivated corruption types at five severity levels, we measure segmentation accuracy, calibration, failure detection, and selective prediction coverage. On clean data, Attention U-Net achieves a whole-tumor Dice of 0.990; under severe Gaussian noise, its performance falls to 0.089. Predictive uncertainty rises with degradation and tracks segmentation error (Pearson r = 0.53 under severity-3 Gaussian noise), allowing us to flag failures with an AUROC of 0.843. These results argue for uncertainty-aware inference as a practical safety layer in physician-in-the-loop radiology workflows. We release the code, trained models, and evaluation protocol to support direct reproduction.

</details>

*Hai Son Nguyen,Duong Ngoc Vu,Trong-Nghia Nguyen,Bien Tran Van,Van-Dem Pham,Trang Mai Xuan,Huan Vu,Thien Van Luong*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰椎间盘退变的自动分级对于客观量化与下背痛相关的结构变化至关重要，然而基线模型在作者的数据上表现不佳，亟需克服这些局限性。

Method: 1) 提出Cross-sequence Attention Spine (CrossSpine)框架，采用跨序列注意力机制在不同空间尺度上自适应融合不同MRI序列的特征；2) 构建了用于自动化Pfirrmann分级的数据集；3) 提出IVD-aware分类技术，整合解剖学椎间盘层级信息，使模型学习特定级别的退变先验。

Result: 相较于基线模型，CrossSpine在Macro F1分数上相对提升超过125%，Mean AUPRC提升99%，Mean AUROC提升36%。

Conclusion: 所提出的CrossSpine框架在腰椎间盘退变自动分级任务上展现出显著的优越性，验证了跨序列特征融合与解剖层级信息引入的有效性。

Abstract: Automated grading of Lumbar Disc Degeneration is essential for the objective quantification of structural changes associated with low back pain. Observing that baseline models underperformed on our data, we propose a framework designed to overcome these limitations. First, we present the Cross-sequence Attention Spine (CrossSpine) framework, a novel architecture that employs a cross-sequence attention mechanism to adaptively fuse features from different MRI sequences at multiple spa- tial scales. Second, we contribute a meticulously curated dataset aimed at automated Pfirrmann grading. Finally, we introduce an IVD-aware classification technique that integrates anatomical disc-level information, enabling the model to learn level-specific degeneration priors. Our experi- ments demonstrate the superiority of this approach: CrossSpine achieved a relative improvement exceeding 125% in the Macro F1 score, while boosting the Mean AUPRC by 99% and the Mean AUROC by 36% com- pared to the baseline.

</details>


### [26] [Open Your Model's Eyes: Video and Context-Aware Multimodal Backchannel Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22729)
*Min-Jae Kim,Jun-Yeong Moon,Mujeen Sung,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAMA-BC框架，通过多层级多模态对齐（MMA）利用视觉信息（面部表情、手势）进行回指预测，在对话上下文对齐（MMA-CA）和回指对齐（MMA-BA）两阶段训练后，显著优于现有方法，特别在识别复杂回指（如共情）方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回指预测方法仅依赖音频和文本，忽略了面部表情、手势等关键视觉线索以及更广泛的对话上下文，而这些信息对于准确预测回指（尤其是复杂回指如共情）至关重要。

Method: 提出CAMA-BC框架，包含两阶段多层级多模态对齐（MMA）：1) 上下文对齐（MMA-CA）：利用未标注对话视频捕获对话上下文；2) 回指对齐（MMA-BA）：针对回指预测任务对表示进行微调。

Result: CAMA-BC显著优于现有方法及简单的多模态基线，在识别共情等复杂回指方面效果尤为突出。

Conclusion: 融合视觉信息的多模态对齐方法能有效提升回指预测性能，尤其是在处理复杂情态回指时具有明显优势，证明了对话上下文和视觉线索在回指预测中的重要性。

Abstract: Backchannels, which signal listener states like empathy and understanding, are fundamental to natural human interaction. However, current approaches rely solely on audio and text. This omits crucial visual cues, such as facial expressions and gestures, as well as broader conversational contexts, which are necessary for accurate prediction. In this paper, we introduce Context-Aware Multimodal Alignment for Backchannel Prediction (CAMA-BC), a novel framework that leverages visual information through Multi-Layer Multimodal Alignment (MMA). Our alignment process comprises two stages. First, Context Alignment (MMA-CA) utilizes unlabeled dialogues with videos to capture conversational contexts. Next, Backchannel Alignment (MMA-BA) fine-tunes the representations specifically for backchannel prediction. Experimental results show that CAMA-BC significantly outperforms both existing methods and simple multimodal baselines, with particular effectiveness in recognizing complex backchannels such as empathy.

</details>


### [27] [Calibration-Free 3D Multi-Camera People Tracking for Indoor Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22731)
*Ponleur Veng,Dominique Vaufreydaz,Phutphalla Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需相机标定的多摄像头3D人员追踪框架，利用深度基础模型直接从视觉数据推断几何结构，在AI City Challenge 2024上达到53.13% HOTA分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCPT依赖精确的内外参标定，而手动标定是从非约束视频档案生成大规模数据集的主要瓶颈。为消除这一依赖，论文探索完全基于视觉数据的无标定3D追踪方案。

Method: 框架集成YOLOX（无锚点检测）、BoT-SORT（鲁棒追踪）、OsNet（全尺度外观嵌入）、HRNet（姿态估计）以及VGGT（基于Transformer的几何重建）。采用姿态引导的3D提升策略将头部关键点投影到重建流形上，避免地面单应性依赖。全局身份关联采用分层凝聚聚类，结合联合外观-几何代价与严格速度门控。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2024上，无需真实标定矩阵即可达到53.13%的HOTA分数。

Conclusion: 建立了纯视觉3D追踪的强基线，证明了无需手动标定即可实现有效的多摄像头3D人员追踪。

Abstract: Multi-Camera People Tracking (MCPT) traditionally relies on precise intrinsic and extrinsic camera calibration to project 2D detections into a unified 3D world coordinate system.However, manual calibration constitutes a major bottleneck in large-scale dataset generation from unconstrained video archives.  This work proposes a unified calibration-free 3D MCPT framework that infers geometric structure directly from visual data using deep foundation models.  The system integrates anchor-free detection (YOLOX), robust tracking (BoT-SORT), omni-scale appearance embedding (OsNet), pose estimation (HRNet via MMPose), and transformer-based geometric reconstruction using the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT). A pose-guided 3D lifting strategy projects head keypoints onto a reconstructed manifold, eliminating dependence on ground-plane homography. Global identity association is formulated as hierarchical agglomerative clustering under a joint appearance-geometry cost with strict velocity gating. Evaluation on the AI City Challenge 2024 demonstrates a HOTA score of 53.13% without access to ground-truth calibration matrices, establishing a strong baseline for purely vision-based 3D tracking.

</details>


### [28] [Generative Augmentation for EEG Motor Imagery Classification: A Class-Conditional VAE with Cycle-Consistent Decoder Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22733)
*Matei Moldoveanu,Alain Sirois,Claire Ben Ali,Fabien Lotte,Florian Yger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究使用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）生成合成运动想象脑电图（MI-EEG）数据以增强下游分类器性能。在Zhou数据集上，该模型通过协方差约束和交替训练策略生成标签一致的合成信号。评估结果表明，合成EEG对MDM分类器有微小提升，但整体增强效果有限且依赖于具体分类器，难以作为真实EEG的替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成模型能否提供有效的合成运动想象脑电图样本，从而提升独立下游分类器的分类准确率，解决真实MI-EEG数据稀缺的问题。

Method: 采用类条件变分自编码器（CVAE），集成潜在空间分类器，在Zhou运动想象数据集上训练。利用学习到的每类先验分布作为生成器进行采样和解码，生成标签一致的合成信号。引入协方差矩阵约束以保持协方差结构，采用交替训练策略（普通VAE训练与解码器聚焦阶段交替进行）增强生成通路。

Result: 在用户内（跨被试60/20/20划分）和跨用户（留一被试法LOSO）两种评估协议下，对CSP+LDA、TGSP+SVM、MDM、EEGNet四种分类流水线进行测试。结果表明：合成EEG仅对MDM分类器的点估计有提升作用，整体增益较小且依赖于具体分类器。

Conclusion: CVAE生成的合成EEG最有价值之处在于提供具有类结构和协方差特性的数据，而非替代真实原始EEG。数据增强的效果保守——观察到的提升幅度小且分类器依赖性强。

Abstract: We investigate whether a generative model can supply useful synthetic motor-imagery (MI) electroencephalography (EEG) trials that improve the accuracy of independent downstream classifiers. We train a class-conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) with an integrated latent classifier on the Zhou motor-imagery dataset, using the learned per-class prior as a generator: sampling the prior for a given label and decoding it into a synthetic, label-consistent signal. A constraint on the covariance matrix of the generated data encourages preservation of covariance structure, and the model is trained with a schedule that alternates ordinary VAE training with a decoder-focused phase that sharpens the generative pathway used for augmentation. We measure the effect of adding synthetic trials to the training set under two evaluation protocols -- within-user (pooled 60/20/20 split across subjects) and cross-user (leave-one-subject-out, LOSO) -- across four representative EEG classification pipelines: Common Spatial Patterns with Linear Discriminant Analysis (CSP+LDA), tangent-space features with a Support Vector Machine (TGSP+SVM), Minimum Distance to Riemannian Mean (MDM), and a neural network based on EEGNetv4 (henceforth EEGNet). Results are aggregated across independent augmentation draws, random seeds (within-user), or leave-one-subject-out folds (cross-user), with uncertainty reported as 95\% confidence intervals (Student's $t$-distribution) computed over per-seed/per-fold averages. We find that synthetic EEG from the CVAE is most credible as a source of class-structured, covariance-like data rather than as a substitute for real raw EEG: it can raise the point estimate for MDM, but the broader augmentation claim remains conservative -- observed gains are small and classifier-dependent.

</details>


### [29] [Fast Fourier Convolutional GAN for 30 m Clear-Sky Land Surface Temperature Gap-Free Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22734)
*Marwa Alfouly,Smajil Halilovic,Nils Bochow,Thomas Hamacher,Niklas Boers,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态快速傅里叶卷积生成对抗网络（MFFC-GAN），利用SAR等辅助数据重建Landsat 30米分辨率卫星影像中云污染像素，生成无间隙的晴空地表温度产品，在超过70%缺失区域下仍有效，场景平均RMSE的IQR稳定在0.8K-1.8K之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星衍生的LST数据因云覆盖导致严重数据缺失，限制了其应用价值。现有方法缺乏可扩展且自适应的管道来生成无间隙LST数据集，尤其是精细空间分辨率观测中大面积缺失区域的重建尤为困难。

Method: 提出多模态快速傅里叶卷积生成对抗网络（MFFC-GAN），利用快速傅里叶卷积（FFC）实现全局感受野，以卫星观测和合成孔径雷达（SAR）数据组成的多模态数据堆栈作为引导，对Landsat 30米影像中云污染像素进行重建。

Result: 在所有LST分位数上，场景平均RMSE的四分位距（IQR）稳定在0.8K-1.8K之间；该方法能恢复超过70%云诱导缺失的大面积区域。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效重建精细分辨率LST影像中的云污染像素，生成无间隙晴空产品，且依赖的辅助数据在近全球尺度上易于获取，具有良好的可扩展性和适应性。

Abstract: Satellite-derived Land Surface Temperature (LST) provides spatially comprehensive data that ground stations cannot match. However, its utility is frequently limited by severe data gaps due to the presence of clouds. As LST is essential for understanding land-atmosphere interactions, numerous methods have been proposed to address this challenge. Yet, the development of a scalable and adaptable pipeline for generating gap-free LST datasets and reconstructing cloud-contaminated pixels remains challenging. Moreover, the reconstruction of extensive missing regions in fine-spatial-resolution observations is particularly difficult. To address this challenge, we propose a Multimodal Fast Fourier Convolutional GAN for reconstructing cloud-contaminated pixels in fine-resolution (30 m) Landsat imagery to generate gap-free clear-sky LST products. The method leverages Fast Fourier Convolution to enable a global receptive field across the image, and is guided by a stack of data consisting of satellite observations and Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) data. Across all LST quantiles, the interquartile range of scene-averaged RMSE (computed over reconstructed pixels) is consistently between 0.8 K and 1.8 K. The proposed approach enables the recovery of extensive missing regions, including scenes with more than 70% cloud-induced gaps, while relying on auxiliary data that are readily available at a near-global scale.

</details>


### [30] [Benchmarking the Domain Gap: Model Selection Instability Under Domain Shift in Video Capsule Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22736)
*Dan Hanson,Debesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视频胶囊内镜（VCE）分类模型在跨数据集泛化中的严重不一致性：在源数据集（Kvasir-Capsule）上表现最佳的骨干网络，在目标数据集（Galar和CV2024）上表现排名不稳定，且两个非源目标之间仅存在微弱的一致性。因此，单一数据集评估无法可靠预测跨源部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VCE分类通常在单一数据集内评估，但临床部署需要跨采集源、标注策略和患者群体的鲁棒性。作者旨在检验这种领域差距并评估跨数据集的泛化能力。

Method: 使用Kvasir-Capsule、CV2024和Galar（共享标签子集）三个数据集。在Kvasir-Capsule官方划分上微调多种通用预训练骨干网络，并在两个非源目标上评估相同的检查点，构建共享标签决策空间进行系统性跨域评估。

Result: 源域排名对目标域的预测价值具有依赖性：Kvasir-Capsule排名与Galar更一致，与CV2024一致性较弱；两个非源目标之间仅弱相关；最强源域骨干在一个目标上领先，在另一个上排名居中；CV2024训练集验证了这种目标依赖的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 胶囊内镜模型选择应报告跨目标排名稳定性而非单数据集峰值性能，单一评估目标无法可靠预测其他目标上的表现。

Abstract: Video capsule endoscopy (VCE) classification is typically evaluated within a single dataset, yet clinical deployment demands robustness across acquisition sources, labeling policies, and patient populations. We examine this gap using Kvasir-Capsule, Capsule Vision 2024 (CV2024), and a shared-label subset of Galar. We fine-tune a suite of general-domain pretrained backbones on the official Kvasir-Capsule folds under a standardized protocol and evaluate the same checkpoints on two non-source targets within a documented shared-label decision space. We find that the predictive value of in-domain ranking is target-dependent: Kvasir-Capsule ranking aligns more closely with Galar than with CV2024, while the two non-source targets agree only weakly. Consequently, the strongest in-domain backbone leads on one target yet falls to mid-pack on the other, and no single evaluation target reliably predicts the others. A second CV2024-trained configuration set reproduces this target-dependent instability. We conclude that capsule endoscopy model selection should report cross-target ranking stability rather than peak single-dataset performance.

</details>


### [31] [Cortex: Compact Behavior Cloning for Quake with Frozen Visual Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22739)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过行为克隆（Behavioral Cloning）训练了一个名为 Cortex 的 Quake 游戏策略，该策略使用冻结的 DINOv3 编码器和六层 Transformer，在无强化学习或显式记忆的情况下，基于 Pixels2Play 数据集中的 474.7 小时人类演示进行训练。Cortex 虽未完成关卡，但能可靠地到达多个关键区域并取得击杀，相较 P2P-150M 和 NitroGen 基线表现更深。消融实验表明密集视觉令牌有助于战斗和生存，但离线指标与实际游戏表现不完全一致，剩余失败归因于协变量偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在视觉丰富的第一人称游戏中，一个刻意简化的行为克隆策略（不含强化学习或显式记忆）能够达到何种水平，从而明确行为克隆的边界和局限性，为后续引入强化学习或记忆模块提供基准和动机。

Method: 使用 DINOv3 作为冻结的视觉编码器，6层 Transformer（共10.98M可训练参数）作为策略网络；在 Pixels2Play 数据集的 Quake 子集（6,849段录制、474.7小时、17.09M帧）上训练，每4帧为一个窗口，单次训练 epoch 使用517,048个窗口，策略头优化耗时3.3分钟（RTX 5080）。评估方式为两批各20次随机120秒的 Quake E1M1 关卡运行。

Result: Cortex 未完成关卡，但所有 episode 均到达起始门、按钮房和门降区；每批中19/20的 episode 至少取得一次击杀。相比 P2P-150M 和 NitroGen 检查点在相同时间控制下表现更深。消融实验显示：密集视觉令牌提升战斗与生存；更长的优化和简单动作历史改善离线指标但未持续改善实际游戏表现。

Conclusion: 纯粹的行为克隆在复杂第一人称游戏中能达到一定水平（可靠到达关键区域、获得击杀），但受限于协变量偏移（covariate shift）无法完成整个关卡，表明需要针对性纠正数据或引入强化学习等在线交互机制来突破该瓶颈。

Abstract: We study how far a deliberately simple behavioral-cloning policy can progress in a visually rich first-person game before adding reinforcement learning or explicit memory. Cortex is a compact Quake policy with 10.98 million trainable parameters in a six-layer transformer over a frozen DINOv3 encoder. It is trained on the Quake subset of the public Pixels2Play corpus: 6,849 recordings (about 474.7 hours), represented as 17.09 million cached decision frames with keyboard and mouse actions. One sampled training epoch uses 517,048 four-frame windows and takes 3.3 minutes of policy-head optimization on one RTX 5080, excluding one-time feature extraction. We evaluate two independent batches of 20 stochastic, 120-second episodes on Quake E1M1. Cortex does not complete the level, but every episode reaches the opening door, button room, and gate descent; 19 of 20 episodes in each batch record at least one kill. Under the same time-controlled harness, released P2P-150M and NitroGen checkpoints remain shallower in five matched-duration episodes each. These comparisons are limited by small reference samples and different native interfaces. Ablations show that denser visual tokens improve combat and survival, while longer optimization and naive action history improve offline metrics without consistently improving play. The remaining failures are consistent with covariate shift and motivate targeted corrective data. We release the policy implementation, checkpoint, and a representative rollout.

</details>

*Vinceline Bertrand,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断差距框架（diagnostic gap framework），用于评估多阶段医学影像流程中每个阶段是否保留了临床相关信息。通过在不同保真度阶梯（VQ-VAE-GAN、VAE-GAN、扩散模型）上评估决策保持和解释保持，发现诊断差距是可测量的重建保真度的函数，而非重建本身的固有代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准评估指标无法有效衡量多阶段弱监督医学影像流程中每个阶段是否保留了临床相关信息，需要一种能够评估决策保持和解释保持的工具。

Method: 提出诊断差距框架，通过保真度阶梯（三种类条件重建器：VQ-VAE-GAN、VAE-GAN、扩散SDEdit，覆盖LPIPS 0.029–0.584的二十倍范围），在病变ROI裁剪区域上评估重建对决策保持（AUC变化）和解释保持（HiResCAM、Grad-CAM++归因相似性）的影响。

Result: 在自动编码器保真度下，决策和解释均保持良好（AUC变化在±0.005以内，归因相似性高）；在扩散保真度下，两者均崩溃（合并AUC下降0.253，肿块-病理AUC低于随机水平）。

Conclusion: 诊断差距是重建保真度可测量的函数，而非重建本身的固有代价；该框架提供了一种与架构无关的工具，用于识别多阶段流程在何处以及何时丢失诊断信号。

Abstract: Weakly supervised pipelines for medical imaging have become increasingly popular over the years. These systems often include multiple stages and components, such as reconstruction, generation, and localization, yet standard evaluation metrics provide limited insight into whether clinically relevant information is preserved across each stage. We present the diagnostic gap framework, a practical evaluation tool that measures decision preservation and explanation preservation as a function of measured reconstruction fidelity. To isolate the effect of reconstruction from localization, we evaluate on curated lesion ROI crops using a fidelity ladder of three class-conditional reconstructors---VQ-VAE-GAN, VAE-GAN, and diffusion (SDEdit)---spanning a twenty-fold range in perceptual distance (LPIPS 0.029--0.584). At autoencoder fidelity, both decision and explanation are preserved: AUC changes remain within $\pm$0.005 and attribution similarity (HiResCAM, Grad-CAM++) stays high. At diffusion fidelity, both collapse: pooled AUC drops by 0.253 and mass-pathology AUC falls below chance. The diagnostic gap is thus a measurable function of reconstruction fidelity rather than an intrinsic cost of reconstruction, and the framework provides an architecture-agnostic instrument for identifying when and where multi-stage pipelines lose diagnostic signal.

</details>


### [33] [AI-generated Images Challenge Visual Trust in High-risk Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22745)
*Yi-Zhi Wang,Yichen Xiao,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao,Yichao Du,Pengfei Fang,Shimin Di,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeIMG是一个面向公共与个人安全场景的合成图像检测基准，包含12个安全场景、人类标注的异常区域及解释。评估发现，最强的VLM仅识别49.5%的生成图像，最佳专用检测器仅33.1%，远低于人类的81.7%。模型解释仅覆盖29.8%的人类标注异常，对常识冲突和物理不一致的覆盖率分别降至15.0%和12.0%，且检测性能在图像降质后进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测基准很少关注公共安全和个人安全场景中的合成图像，而这些场景中误导性视觉内容可能带来重大风险。需要建立一个安全导向的基准来评估检测器在实际安全场景中的表现。

Method: 构建SafeIMG基准，涵盖12个公共与个人安全场景，使用GPT Image 2生成图像。提供人类标注的异常区域定位和解释（包括局部伪影、常识冲突和物理不一致）。评估专用合成图像检测器和视觉语言模型。

Result: 最强VLM识别率49.5%，最佳专用检测器33.1%，人类81.7%。模型解释仅覆盖29.8%的人类标注异常，对常识冲突覆盖率15.0%，物理不一致12.0%。传播导致的图像降质进一步降低检测性能。

Conclusion: 当前检测器在公共与个人安全场景中缺乏准确性、解释对齐性和鲁棒性，无法可靠评估AI生成图像。

Abstract: Rapid advances in image generation are eroding the evidentiary value of visual content in settings where authenticity can affect public safety and personal reputation. Yet existing detection benchmarks rarely examine synthetic images in public- and individual-safety contexts, where misleading visual content may carry substantial risks. Here we introduce SafeIMG, a safety-oriented benchmark spanning 12 public- and individual-safety scenarios generated using GPT Image 2. Unlike benchmarks centred on generic imagery and image-level labels, SafeIMG evaluates not only whether detectors recognise synthetic images, but also whether their decisions reflect human-identified anomalies. To this end, SafeIMG provides human annotations that localise suspicious regions and explain local artefacts and higher-level commonsense or physical inconsistencies. We evaluate specialized synthetic-image detectors and vision-language models (VLMs), and find that neither provides reliable detection. The strongest VLM identifies only 49.5% of generated images, whereas the best specialised detector identifies 33.1%, compared with 81.7% accuracy for human evaluators. Model explanations cover only 29.8\% of human-annotated anomalies and predominantly capture local defects in text, faces and hands. Their coverage falls to 15.0% for commonsense conflicts and 12.0% for physical inconsistencies, while detection performance deteriorates further after dissemination-induced image degradation. These findings show that current detectors lack the accuracy, explanatory alignment and robustness needed to evaluate AI-generated images reliably across public- and individual-safety settings.

</details>

*Hongruixuan Chen,He Huang,Haifeng Wang,Jian Song,Junjue Wang,Weihao Xuan,Hamish Mitchell,Jiepan Li,Wei He,Liangpei Zhang,Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Jiazhen Zhao,Lei Hu,Ting Hu,Hongyan Zhang,Gregory Angelides,Miriam Cha,Clifford Broni-Bediako,Junshi Xia,Taylor Perron,Naoto Yokoya*

TL;DR: BRIGHT Challenge 评估了仅使用灾前高分辨率光学影像和灾后SAR影像进行全天候建筑物损毁制图的性能。该挑战扩展了BRIGHT数据集，包含约29.1万栋建筑物、16个灾害事件。最终阶段在两个2025年未见事件上评估，两个获胜方案达到mAP 0.182和0.181，约为基线（0.021）的8.7倍，但远低于域内最佳得分（0.513）。获胜方案均偏好模态特定编码、晚期融合及光学主导的定位-识别分离。跨事件泛化和稳定严重性判别是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后快速响应需要及时的建筑级损毁信息，但灾后光学影像常因云层、烟雾或黑暗而不可用。因此需要探索基于SAR影像的任意天气下建筑物损毁制图方法，评估当前技术在实际跨事件场景中的泛化能力。

Method: BRIGHT Challenge使用灾前亚米级光学影像和灾后SAR影像，要求参与者检测并分割每栋建筑物，赋予三种互斥损毁标签之一。数据集包含约29.1万栋建筑、16个灾害事件（7种灾害类型）。最终阶段在两个2025年未见事件（加州野火和牙买加飓风）上评估。

Result: 157名参与者提交1,289次，46支队伍进入最终阶段。获胜方案mAP为0.182和0.181，约为基线（0.021）的8.7倍，但远低于域内最佳得分（0.513）。跨阶段排名变化显著，性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 跨事件泛化和稳定严重性判别是当前建筑损毁制图的主要挑战。获胜方案偏好模态特定编码、晚期融合和光学主导的定位-识别分离策略。

Abstract: Rapid post-disaster response requires timely, building-level information on whether structures remain intact, are damaged, or are destroyed. Post-event optical imagery, however, may be unavailable because of cloud, smoke, or darkness. The Bright Challenge evaluated all-weather building damage mapping from a submeter-resolution pre-event optical image and a post-event SAR image. Participants were required to detect and delineate each building and assign exactly one of three mutually exclusive damage labels. The challenge extended the globally distributed \textsc{Bright} dataset with instance-level annotations for about 291,000 buildings across 16 disaster events spanning seven disaster types. The final phase was evaluated exclusively on two 2025 events absent from training: a wildfire event in California and a hurricane in Jamaica. A total of 157 participants made 1,289 submissions, and 46 teams entered the final phase. The two winning solutions achieved test mAPs of 0.182 and 0.181, approximately 8.7 times the public baseline of 0.021, but remained far below the best in-domain holdout score of 0.513. Across teams ranked in both phases, performance declined sharply and the rank order changed substantially. The two leading solutions independently favored modality-specific encoding, staged or late optical--SAR fusion, and an optical-dominant separation of building localization from damage recognition. The winning method additionally used scene-aware threshold adjustment and pseudo-label adaptation. These results identify cross-event generalization and stable severity discrimination as the principal remaining challenges. All data, annotations, baseline code, and winning solutions are publicly available at https://github.com/ChenHongruixuan/BRIGHT.

</details>
