<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后残留肿瘤的自动分割对早期发现复发至关重要，但现有Transformer架构（如SwinUNETR）在跨临床协议泛化时性能显著下降，标准广义Dice损失（GDL）在域偏移下不稳定（WL Dice从0.88降至0.73），亟需提升模型鲁棒性和跨域泛化能力。

Method: 1) 脑掩膜百分位归一化预处理；2) 体素级对比学习增强特征判别性；3) 提出子空间感知类别注意力（SACA）模块，在瓶颈特征处重校准以提升增强肿瘤（ET）敏感性；4) 将上述改进与nnU-Net集成进行集成学习。

Result: SACA模块使内部验证集上ET灵敏度提升8%（相对提升9.1%）；集成所有改进后各稳定配置的WL Dice达0.94；SACA变体集成在MU-GLIOMA-POST数据集上取得最优边界误差HD95为2.92 mm。

Conclusion: 所提出的归一化、对比学习与SACA注意力机制能有效缓解域偏移问题，提升术后胶质瘤分割的跨域泛化性能，集成策略可进一步逼近最优分割精度。

Abstract: Tracking residual tumor after surgery is essential for catching recurrence early, but automating post-operative glioma segmentation remains a difficult task. Although transformer-based architectures, such as SwinUNETR, achieved impressive results, few studies test how well they generalize across clinical protocols. In this paper, we conduct an ablation study on the MU-GLIOMA-POST and UCSF-ALPTDG datasets and show that the standard Generalized Dice Loss (GDL) is unstable under domain shift: the Whole Lesion (WL) Dice drops from 0.88 on the internal validation set to 0.73 on the external UCSF test set. To address this, we pair brain-masked percentile normalization with voxel-level contrastive learning. We also propose a Subspace-Aware Class Attention (SACA) module that re-calibrates the bottleneck features and raises Enhancing Tumor (ET) sensitivity by 8% (9.1% relative improvement) on internal validation. Ensembling these refinements with nnU-Net brings every stable configuration to a WL Dice of 0.94, and the SACA variant ensemble achieves the best boundary error (HD95) of 2.92 mm on MU-GLIOMA-POST.

</details>


### [36] [Beyond Error-vs-Discard Characteristic: Toward Stable and Reliable Evaluation for Face Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22752)
*Bhavesh Wani,Žiga Babnik,Vitomir Štruc,Philipp Terhörst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了FIQA领域广泛使用的EDC评估协议存在测试集偏移和阈值漂移两大根本性局限，并提出基于丢弃的EDC变体和基于排序的RCE指标作为更可靠、可比较的评估方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FIQA评估主要依赖EDC协议，但该协议存在未被充分认识的根本性缺陷（测试集偏移和阈值漂移），限制了评估结果的可靠性和方法之间的可比性。

Method: 1) 分析并证明了EDC协议的两大局限性：Test-Set Divergence（丢弃低质量样本后测试集分布改变）和Threshold Drift（识别阈值随丢弃比例漂移）；2) 提出基于丢弃的EDC变体以缓解上述问题；3) 提出基于排名的RCE指标，在固定决策阈值下对整个测试集进行评估，无需丢弃样本。

Result: 在5个数据集、4个面部识别模型和15种SOTA FIQA方法上的大量实验验证了EDC的局限性，并证明了所提方法在实现更可靠和可比较评估方面的有效性。

Conclusion: EDC协议的局限源于评估协议本身而非生物特征模态，因此本文的发现和提出的解决方案具有超越面部图像领域的更广泛适用性，可推广至通用生物特征质量评估。

Abstract: Face Image Quality Assessment (FIQA) aims to estimate the utility of facial images for reliable recognition. The evaluation of FIQA methods is predominantly based on the Error-versus-Discard Characteristic (EDC), which evaluates performance by progressively discarding low-quality samples and measuring recognition error on the retained subset. In this work, we demonstrate that the widely used EDC protocol has fundamental limitations: Test-Set Divergence and Threshold Drift, which together limit the reliability and comparability of FIQA methods. To address this, we propose discard-based EDC variants and a rank-based Rank Consistency Evaluation (RCE) metric that operates on the entire test set without discarding samples, using a fixed decision threshold. Extensive experiments on five datasets, four face recognition models, and 15 state-of-the-art FIQA methods demonstrate both the limitations of EDC and the effectiveness of the proposed approaches in enabling a more reliable and comparable evaluation. Despite evaluated on face images only, the limitations arise from the EDC protocol rather than the biometric modality, suggesting a broader applicability to biometric quality assessment in general.

</details>


### [37] [Real-time Reconstruction of Human Visual Perception from fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22753)
*Rishab S. Iyer,Jiaxin Cindy Tu,Cesar Kadir Torrico Villanueva,Anish Mahishi,Ross P. Kempner,Jacob S. Prince,Ernest W. Lo,Akash Bhowmick,Hritik Arasu,Amaar Chughtai,Elizabeth A. McDevitt,Paul S. Scotti,Kenneth A. Norman*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实时兼容的改进版MindEye2管道，能够在fMRI实时闭环神经反馈中实现自然图像感知的细粒度解码。通过RT-Cloud云平台，研究者在真实扫描中实现了单试次视觉感知的秒级解码，并通过模拟分析揭示了离线到实时性能变化的关键因素。该工作证明了将先进fMRI解码管道部署于实时分析的可行性，为脑机接口在科学发现和临床治疗中的应用铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时fMRI的分析方法在复杂度上落后于离线fMRI解码的最新进展，主要原因是计算限制——大多数先进解码管道无法在数秒内完成实时处理，且不能利用后续时段采集的数据。

Method: 对计算密集的最先进自然图像重建管道MindEye2进行实时兼容性改造；利用RT-Cloud开源云平台执行实时扫描，在图像呈现后数秒内解码单试次视觉感知；通过模拟分析量化离线分析到实时分析的性能变化驱动因素。

Result: 在实时场景下仍然实现了可靠的细粒度解码；证明了先进fMRI解码管道可以部署在实时分析中。

Conclusion: 该工作是概念验证，表明将强大的fMRI解码管道用于实时分析是可行的，为其在脑机接口、科学发现和临床治疗中的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Real-time closed-loop neurofeedback based on functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has led to important scientific and clinical advances. However, the sophistication of the analysis methods used in real-time fMRI lags behind the state-of-the-art in fMRI decoding, largely due to computational factors: Most advanced decoding pipelines do not fit within the envelope of real-time processing, where the analysis needs to be conducted in a matter of seconds and without leveraging data acquired later in the session. Here, we present a real-time compatible adaptation of a computationally intensive state-of-the-art pipeline for reconstructing perceived natural images (MindEye2), and we demonstrate that reliable fine-grained decoding is still achievable in this setting. Using RT-Cloud, an open-source, scalable cloud-based platform, we performed a real-time scan where we decoded single-trial visual perception within seconds after an image was shown to the participant. Finally, we use simulated analyses to document the factors driving changes in performance from offline to real-time analysis. This work serves as a proof-of-concept that it is feasible to deploy these powerful fMRI decoding pipelines in real-time analysis, paving the way for their use in brain-computer interfaces for scientific discovery and clinical treatment.

</details>


### [38] [Cheap Probes Predict Expensive Training in 3D-CT Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22771)
*Renjie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用廉价的冻结编码器探针替代昂贵的全参数微调，用于3D CT视觉-语言模型中图像编码器与令牌压缩方案的联合搜索，在初步实验中与微调结果的相关性达到r≈0.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D CT视觉-语言模型中，选择图像编码器及其令牌压缩方案（含多种编码器、压缩方式和令牌预算）需要大量组合搜索。传统方法需对每种组合进行完整的LLM微调，计算成本过高，亟需一种廉价替代方案来快速筛选候选组合。

Method: 构建基于图像锚定的探测基准（覆盖编码器×压缩方案的单元格），引入两个验证门控机制：scale-sanity（确保属性良好缩放）和probe-separability（确保属性可解码）。在该基准上比较多种读出头，并将每个探针与其匹配的下游任务配对进行初步研究。

Result: 廉价探针与昂贵微调在候选排序上高度一致，已测量的单元格上Spearman相关系数约为r≈0.95。该方法被明确定义为序数预测器（排序预测）而非精确估计器。

Conclusion: 如果这一发现经得起验证，编码器和压缩方案的选择可通过冻结令牌探针在数分钟内完成筛选，仅需对最终候选组合进行完整的全参数训练，大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Picking the frozen image encoder for a 3D~CT vision--language model (VLM), together with the token-compression scheme on top of it, is a search over many candidates. There are several encoders, several ways to compress their tokens, and several token budgets, and the combinations grow fast. Comparing them the usual way means fine-tuning a large language model (LLM) on each combination, and running the whole sweep this way needs far more compute than most groups can spend. We ask whether a cheap probe on the encoder's cached embeddings can stand in for that comparison. We build an image-grounded probing benchmark over (encoder $\times$ compression) cells, with clinical attribute families and two validation gates, scale-sanity and probe-separability, that keep each attribute well-scaled and decodable. These gates are the main methodological contribution. On this benchmark we compare a range of read-out heads, and in a preliminary study we pair each probe with its matched downstream task. The early signal is encouraging: the cheap probe orders the candidates in close agreement with expensive fine-tuning, at about $r\approx0.95$ on the cells measured so far. We read this as an ordinal claim, a ranking predictor rather than an exact estimate, and we are explicit about where it stays preliminary. If it holds up, encoder and compression choices can be screened in minutes with frozen-token probes, with full training spent only on the finalists.

</details>


### [39] [Hybrid Semantic and Spectral Ensemble for Robust Synthetic Image Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22808)
*Md. Ajwad Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像模型合成图像的来源归因问题，提出了一种融合语义深度学习与数学取证特征提取的双分支集成框架。语义分支使用EfficientNet-B0（EMA+标签平滑），取证分支从高通噪声残差中提取126维数学特征（SVD谱、LBP等），经截断SVD降维后由XGBoost分类。在含55%退化测试集的数据集上达到95.60%的私榜准确率，且整个流程无需GPU，标准CPU上6.5小时内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型的快速发展使得合成图像来源归因成为必要，但训练图像与实际部署图像之间存在分布偏移（如JPEG压缩、模糊等未知后处理操作），现有方法难以应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出双分支集成框架：(1) 语义分支：EfficientNet-B0，结合指数移动平均（EMA）和标签平滑正则化；(2) 取证分支：从高通噪声残差中提取126个数学特征（包括SVD谱特征和局部二值模式LBP），通过截断SVD进行降维，使用XGBoost分类器。两分支结果进行集成。

Result: 在包含10种生成器、55%测试集经后处理退化的数据集上，私榜准确率达到95.60%。

Conclusion: 整个流程无需GPU加速，在标准CPU上端到端执行时间不超过6.5小时，展示了数学取证方法在实际部署中的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of text-to-image (T2I) models has necessitated robust Synthetic Image Source Attribution (SIA) methodologies. A critical challenge in SIA is the distribution shift between pristine training images and real-world deployed images, which undergo unknown post-processing operations such as JPEG compression and blurring. In this work, proposed for the DLMMDD Challenge at ICANN 2026, we introduce a dual-branch ensemble framework fusing Semantic Deep Learning with Mathematical Forensic Feature Extraction. The semantic branch employs EfficientNet-B0 regularized with Exponential Moving Averaging (EMA) and Label Smoothing. The forensic branch extracts 126 mathematical features -- including SVD spectral profiles and Local Binary Patterns -- from high-pass noise residuals, compressed via Truncated SVD and classified with XGBoost. Evaluated on a dataset of 10 generators where 55% of the test set is degraded, our approach achieves a private leaderboard accuracy of 95.60%. Furthermore, the entire pipeline is highly computationally efficient, requiring no GPU acceleration and executing end-to-end on a standard CPU in under 6.5 hours, highlighting the practicality and scalability of mathematical forensics for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [40] [ID-V2V: Identity-Preserving Video Restylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22830)
*Yuancheng Xu,Mingming He,Pablo Salamanca,Li Ma,Yash Kant,Emmett Steven,Paul Debevec,Ning Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ID-V2V框架，通过将身份保持分解为视频重光照问题、视觉编辑传播建模为受控视频合成问题，利用重光照面部区域、面部法向图、编辑关键帧和深度序列作为互补控制信号，在无需配对训练数据的情况下实现身份保持的视频重风格化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉叙事中，人类表演是创意意图和叙事意义的核心，但现有生成式视频模型在实现灵活视觉编辑的同时保持人类身份和表演（包括表情、视线、唇形同步）仍面临挑战，且缺乏配对的训练数据。

Method: 将身份保持与编辑驱动视频合成解耦：身份保持视为视频重光照问题（面部外观和表情不变，仅光照可变），视觉编辑传播建模为编辑关键帧引导的受控视频合成。ID-V2V框架整合四种互补控制信号：重光照面部区域、面部法向图（约束面部相似度和表演）、编辑关键帧和深度序列（实现灵活且时间一致的生成）。训练数据可从单视频构造。

Result: ID-V2V在保持面部相似度和细粒度面部表演方面显著优于现有方法，支持单人和多人场景，输出高视觉质量。

Conclusion: ID-V2V作为一种以人为中心的工具，在现实世界内容生产中具有潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: In visual storytelling, human performances are central to creative intent and narrative meaning. However, preserving human identity and performance while enabling flexible visual edits remains challenging for generative video models. We formalize this challenge as identity-preserving video restylization, which propagates scene, lighting, and style changes specified by an edited keyframe across a source video, while preserving facial likeness and performance, including expressions, eye gaze, and lip synchronization. A key obstacle is the absence of paired training data, as identity-preserving restylized video pairs are rare in real-world settings. To address this, we propose a decoupling of source-grounded identity preservation and edit-driven video synthesis. Our key insight is that facial appearance and expression should remain invariant, with illumination being the primary permissible variation. We therefore cast identity preservation as a video relighting problem, while modeling visual edit propagation as controlled video synthesis guided by the edited keyframe. Building on this formulation, we introduce ID-V2V, a video-to-video generative framework integrating complementary control signals: relit facial regions and facial normal maps tightly constrain facial likeness and performance, while edited keyframes and depth sequences enable flexible and temporally coherent generation. This design enables constructing training pairs from a single video, eliminating the need for scarce paired data. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ID-V2V significantly outperforms existing methods in preserving facial likeness and fine-grained facial performance, supports both single- and multi-subject scenarios, and delivers high visual quality, highlighting its potential as a human-centric tool for real-world content production. The code is available at: https://github.com/Eyeline-Labs/ID-V2V.

</details>


### [41] [Gaze-Anchored Social Net: Decoding Implicit Relations via Joint Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22847)
*Yuqi Hou,Zhuo Chen,Han Hu,Je Woo Kim,Jianbo Jiao,Hyung Jin Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ANCHOR框架，通过目标中心范式联合建模视觉注意与潜在社会关系，利用关系注意力机制和优化协同训练，首次从静态注视模式中稳健解耦和学习隐含的社会层级，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型将注视视为独立同分布量或将社会关系作为后验分类，未能捕捉作为注视行为底层驱动力的社会意图的细微本质。

Method: 提出目标中心范式ANCHOR，包含关系注意力机制捕捉细粒度人际联系、特征调制实现高效多人解析、优化协同（寻找稳定平坦极小值并协调竞争任务梯度）以稳定联合训练。

Result: 在扩展的密集多人标注基准上达到SOTA性能，首次定量证明隐含社会层级可从静态注视模式中稳健解耦并学习。

Conclusion: ANCHOR成功将社会意图作为注视行为的潜在结构支撑进行建模，验证了从静态注视中学习隐含社会层级的可行性。

Abstract: Human gaze does more than point to visual targets; it serves as a subtle indicator of social intent within static images, whereas standard models typically process individuals independently, treating gaze as an i.i.d. quantity or predicting social semantics in isolation. Recent multi-person methods attempt to address this but often treat social relations as rigid, post-hoc classifications decoupled from the gaze estimation process. This oversimplification fails to capture the nuanced nature of social intent, which acts as an underlying driver of gaze behavior rather than a secondary categorical output. We address these limitations by proposing ANCHOR, a target-centric paradigm designed to decode gaze-anchored social intent by modeling the joint distribution of visual attention and latent implicit relations. Our approach surfaces these dependencies as the latent structural scaffolding of gaze behavior. The architecture utilizes a relational attention mechanism to capture fine-grained interpersonal links, leveraging feature-wise modulation for efficient multi-person parsing from a single vision backbone. To stabilize the training of this coupled formulation, we implement an optimization synergy to resolve the inherent conflicts between spatial gaze accuracy and latent social reasoning. This approach ensures robust generalization by seeking stable, flat minima while simultaneously harmonizing competing task gradients. We validate our framework on an extended benchmark featuring dense multi-person annotations and novel social influence rankings. Our results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and provide the first quantitative evidence that implicit social hierarchies can be robustly disentangled and learned directly from static gaze patterns.

</details>


### [42] [Robustifying pathology foundation models via fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22861)
*Alexandre Filiot,Oskar Thaeter,Benoit Schmauch,Lionel Guillou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对病理基础模型的新型微调方法，能同时提升模型对扫描仪和染色差异的鲁棒性及下游任务性能。在10个模型上平均提升鲁棒性23%（PathoROB从0.72到0.87），跨基准性能提升43%，最高分别达72%和76%。已开源Phikon-v2和Midnight-12k的微调版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型生成的tile级表征对扫描仪和染色差异敏感，限制了跨实验室部署的泛化能力，亟需提升模型对采集因素的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种新型微调策略（fine-tuning recipe），在10种不同的病理基础模型上应用，通过实验验证该策略对鲁棒性和下游性能的提升效果。

Result: 平均PathoROB鲁棒性指数从0.72提升至0.87（+23%）；在Patho-Bench、HEST和THUNDER三个基准上整体性能提升43%；单模型最高鲁棒性提升72%（Phikon-v2）、性能提升76%（Midnight-12k）。所有10个模型均无性能折衷。

Conclusion: 所提微调方法能有效提升病理基础模型的跨实验室部署鲁棒性，且与下游性能呈正相关，可推广至多种现有病理基础模型。已公开两个代表性模型的微调版本供社区使用。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (FMs) produce powerful tile-level representations which remain sensitive to scanner and staining variability, undermining deployment across laboratories. We develop a novel fine-tuning recipe that improves the robustness of pathology FMs to acquisition factors. Applied to ten different FMs, our fine-tuning strategy consistently improves robustness for every model as well as downstream performance, with no observed trade-off. On average, it raises the PathoROB robustness index by 23% (from 0.72 to 0.87) and increases the overall cross-benchmark performance by 43% on Patho-Bench, HEST and THUNDER combined, with individual gains reaching up to 72% in robustness (Phikon-v2) and 76% in performance (Midnight-12k). We publicly release the fine-tuned versions of Phikon-v2 (Phaet) and Midnight-12k (Mascaret) at https://huggingface.co/wearewaiv/models.

</details>


### [43] [Spatial-IQ: Deconstructing Spatial Intelligence via Hierarchical Capability Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22864)
*Patrick Rim,Tom Long,Ekta Prashnani,Ruth Rosenholtz,Ben Boudaoud,Peter Xenopoulos,Alex Wong,Joohwan Kim,Jae-Hyun Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Spatial-IQ，一个分层诊断框架，将3D堆叠结构中的物体计数分解为9个感知与认知子任务，用于评估多模态大模型的视觉空间推理能力。实验表明，高性能模型常在目标任务上成功但底层子任务失败，存在捷径行为；基于子任务的CoT监督结合强化学习可显著提升空间一致性和目标任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将多模态大模型作为黑盒评估，无法区分模型在空间推理任务中失败的原因是感知层面的（如识别物体边界）还是认知层面的（如推理遮挡几何结构），亟需能定位性能瓶颈根源的诊断性框架。

Method: 提出Spatial-IQ分层诊断框架，将堆叠3D结构中的物体计数分解为9个感知与认知子任务（按人类空间认知发展阶段组织），并增加心理旋转作为探测目标。使用NVIDIA Isaac Sim程序化生成约8万堆叠3D结构数据集，在三种输出格式（自由文本、多选图像、图像编辑）下评估模型，并引入人类基线。

Result: 高性能模型常在目标任务（物体计数）上成功，但并未成功完成支撑该目标的底层子任务；不同模型保留了不同程度的分层链，常表现出捷径行为，这些被原始目标任务准确率所掩盖。基于分层子任务的CoT监督结合可验证奖励的强化学习，显著提升了子任务间的空间一致性和目标任务准确率。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层分解不仅可作为诊断工具定位模型空间推理失败的根源，还可作为训练信号提升模型的空间推理能力，验证了该分解框架的双重价值。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at visual interpretation but fail on spatial reasoning tasks that humans solve reliably. Existing benchmarks evaluate these models as black boxes, limiting their ability to identify the underlying causes of lower performance: when a model fails a spatial reasoning task, it remains difficult to ascertain whether the hurdle is perceptual, such as recognizing object boundaries, or cognitive, such as reasoning about occlusion to infer hidden geometry. We introduce Spatial-IQ, a hierarchical diagnostic framework that decomposes object counting in stacked 3D structures into 9 perceptual and cognitive sub-tasks organized by the developmental stages of human spatial cognition, with mental rotation as an additional target probe. Using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, we procedurally generated a diverse dataset of roughly 80,000 stacked 3D structures with per-task ground truth. We evaluate models across three output formats (free-response text, multiple-choice images, and image editing) alongside a human baseline. The Spatial-IQ framework shows that top-performing models often succeed at the target task (object counting) without succeeding on the lower-level sub-tasks intended to support it, and that models differ in how much of these hierarchical chains they preserve, often revealing shortcut behavior that raw target-task accuracy alone would obscure. Finally, we demonstrate that training models with chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision over our hierarchical sub-tasks, combined with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, significantly improves both spatial consistency across sub-tasks and target-task accuracy, supporting the value of the proposed decomposition as both a diagnostic tool and a training signal.

</details>


### [44] [AdaKAN: A dual-branch adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold network for medical image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22891)
*Dalia Alzu'bi,Deep Bhattacharyya,Ali Ayub,A. Ben Hamza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaKAN，一种结合卷积与高效KAN模块的自适应Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，采用U型架构同时捕获长距离依赖和细粒度局部特征，在多个医学影像分割数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割因解剖结构复杂性和不同成像模态的差异性而面临挑战，传统卷积和Transformer模型在捕捉长距离依赖与局部细节方面存在局限。

Method: 提出AdaKAN网络，包含：(1) EffiKAN模块，由高效注意力机制和AdaptKAN模块组成；(2) AdaptKAN双分支设计：一支采用Bernstein多项式激活的KAN层实现全局平滑函数逼近，另一支通过投影操作和自适应缩放进行通道级精炼；(3) U型架构结合跳跃连接实现编码-解码的空间信息保留与重建。

Result: 在多个医学影像数据集上的实验表明，AdaKAN在分割精度上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: AdaKAN通过卷积与KAN的协同整合，有效克服了传统分割模型在长距离依赖和局部特征捕捉上的不足，为医学图像分割提供了新的SOTA方案。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is a fundamental task in computer-aided diagnosis, yet it remains challenging due to the complexity of anatomical structures and the variability across imaging modalities. In this paper, we propose AdaKAN, an Adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) that synergistically integrates convolutional operations with a novel efficient KAN (EffiKAN) block, comprised of an efficient attention mechanism and an adaptive KAN (AdaptKAN) module. This module features a dual-branch design: one branch employs a KAN layer with Bernstein polynomial activations for globally smooth and stable function approximation, while the other branch performs channel-wise refinement through projection operations and adaptive scaling. AdaKAN adopts a U-shaped architecture that effectively captures both long-range dependencies and fine-grained local features, overcoming the limitations of conventional convolutional and Transformer-based segmentation models. Skip connections are employed to preserve spatial details during encoding and facilitate accurate reconstruction during decoding. Extensive experiments conducted on diverse medical imaging datasets demonstrate that AdaKAN achieves state-of-the-art performance in segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [45] [Small-Pollinator Detection in Cluttered Field Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22913)
*Onur Onal,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统研究了小目标传粉昆虫检测：对比YOLO与RF-DETR模型，RF-DETR Large在1344分辨率下取得最佳结果（0.405 mAP50:95），检测器选择和输入分辨率比推理复杂度更有效，蜜蜂-食蚜蝇判别是主要瓶颈，提出需在分类决策前进行特征级时间聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田间视频中传粉昆虫检测面临目标小、视觉相似、背景杂乱、模糊和遮挡等挑战，需在单GPU计算预算下进行系统的实证研究以提升检测性能。

Method: 基于BuzzSpot挑战数据集，对比YOLO和RF-DETR模型在不同输入分辨率下的性能；评估切片推理融合、类别门控融合、尺寸路由集成和后处理时序处理等策略。

Result: RF-DETR Large在1344分辨率达到0.405 mAP50:95，优于1120分辨率（0.379）和最佳单模型YOLO26m（0.366）。分辨率提升对小目标和稀有类别（熊蜂、蛾类）增益最大。切片融合、尺寸路由集成和1536分辨率续训未超越该结果，后处理时序处理未改善诊断评估。

Conclusion: 检测器选择和输入分辨率是比推理复杂度更有效的杠杆；蜜蜂-食蚜蝇判别是主要瓶颈，相邻帧难以提供正确分类证据；未来需在分类决策前进行学习式特征级时间聚合。

Abstract: Detecting pollinators in field video is challenging: targets are small, visually similar, and observed against cluttered vegetation under blur and occlusion. We present a systematic empirical study of small-pollinator detection under a practical single-GPU compute budget. Using the BuzzSpot challenge dataset, we compare YOLO and RF-DETR models across input resolutions and evaluate sliced inference, class-gated fusion, size-routed ensembling, and post-hoc temporal processing. RF-DETR Large at 1344-pixel resolution achieved our best hidden-test result, reaching 0.405 mAP50:95 and outperforming the 1120-pixel model (0.379) and the best single-model YOLO26m baseline (0.366). The strongest gains came from adopting RF-DETR and increasing its input resolution, indicating that detector choice and input resolution were more effective levers than added inference-time complexity; the resolution gain was strongest for small objects and the rarer bumblebee and moth classes. Sliced-inference fusion, size-routed ensembling, and warm-started 1536-pixel continuation did not surpass this result, while post-hoc temporal processing did not improve the leaked diagnostic evaluation. Error analysis identified bee-hoverfly discrimination as the clearest remaining bottleneck: neighboring frames rarely supplied correctly classified hoverfly evidence for post-hoc correction. These findings motivate learned feature-level temporal aggregation before the final classification decision.

</details>


### [46] [Controlling Embedding Spaces with Text-Conditioned Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22919)
*Joseph Fioresi,Fabian Caba Heilbron,Pankaj Nathani,Mubarak Shah,Kushal Kafle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种文本条件驱动的视觉嵌入变换方法（ControlEmbed），通过自然语言描述属性类别（如“颜色”“艺术风格”），利用网络生成仿射变换以显式强调指定属性，实现属性级检索与多属性解耦，无需重编码即可操作已有大规模嵌入空间，以近零推理成本达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态嵌入空间（如CLIP）将高层语义压缩为单一向量，导致仅能表达主导语义（如主物体），而抑制了其他重要属性（如拍摄角度、色调）。现有方法缺乏在不重编码的前提下显式控制并提取这些被压制属性的能力。

Method: 以自然语言描述属性类别作为条件输入，训练一个网络生成仿射变换（含缩放与偏移），应用于视觉嵌入以增强指定属性。变换后的嵌入与冻结的原始潜在空间对齐，从而无需重新编码即可利用现有大规模嵌入进行检索与多聚类等属性解耦任务。

Result: 在基于属性的检索和多属性组织任务上达到SOTA性能，且推理成本近乎为零。方法统一高效，适用于已有的大规模嵌入空间。

Conclusion: ControlEmbed提供了一种统一、高效的框架，通过在潜在空间中直接操作实现了对嵌入空间的控制，解决了多模态嵌入中属性信息被主导语义压制的问题，并在多种任务上取得最优结果。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding spaces in models like CLIP enable powerful capabilities such as semantic similarity retrieval and cross-modal zero-shot classification. These embeddings compress high-level semantics into a single vector, which comes at the cost of primarily expressing a dominant semantics like main object while suppressing other important attributes such as camera angle or color tone. We propose a text-conditioned transformation of visual embeddings that makes such attributes explicitly accessible. Given a natural language description of an attribute category (e.g., "color" or "art style"), a network generates an affine transformation that emphasizes the specified attribute. Conditioning on text enables it to learn many attributes simultaneously, accessing them at inference time through an intuitive interface. The network is trained to align transformed embeddings with the frozen latent space, enabling retrieval using existing large-scale embeddings without any re-encoding. When applied to a full set, the same mechanism transforms the latent space for attribute disentanglement tasks such as multi-clustering. By operating directly in latent space, our method provides a unified and efficient framework for controlling embedding spaces, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance across both attribute-based retrieval and multi-attribute organization tasks with near-zero inference cost. Project page: https://joefioresi718.github.io/ControlEmbed_webpage/

</details>


### [47] [Layering Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22924)
*Chun Feng,Bowei Chen,Mengyi Shan,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LVTON，一种支持服装顺序叠加与移除的虚拟试穿方法，通过解耦通用VTON先验和特定叠穿知识，在叠穿和传统VTON基准上均取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的穿搭涉及多层服装的叠加（如外套内搭衬衫）而非单层替换，现有VTON方法仅擅长单层替换，无法处理叠穿或脱衣操作。

Method: 将LVTON解耦为两个能力：(1) 通用VTON先验（变形、身份保持），通过自动数据生成管道从时尚视频中合成训练样本；(2) 特定叠穿知识（叠穿顺序、遮挡推理），在小型专用LVTON数据集上微调模型。

Result: 在LVTON基准上取得SOTA结果；在传统VTON基准上展现出优越的泛化能力，微调后达到新SOTA，且具备零样本能力。

Conclusion: 解耦通用先验与特定知识的策略有效解决了叠穿虚拟试穿难题，同时不牺牲传统单层替换性能。

Abstract: In the real world, fashion is about layering: adding a jacket over a shirt, or a sequence of adding and removing layers, rather than just a single-layer swap. This fundamental real-world task remains a challenge in existing Virtual Try-On (VTON) methods, which excel at single-layer replacement but are not designed to layer or de-layer an existing outfit. This paper proposes Layering Virtual Try-On (LVTON), a layering benchmark and method that preserves an existing outfit while enabling sequential layering. We find that current VTON paradigms are fundamentally ill-equipped for LVTON, as their reliance on cloth-agnostic representations and single-item datasets discards essential layering context. Our key insight is that the LVTON challenge must be disentangled into two distinct competencies: (1) General VTON Priors (e.g., deformation, identity preservation) and (2) Specific Layering Knowledge (e.g., layering order and occlusion reasoning). First, our model obtains general VTON priors by being trained on data produced by an automatic data generation pipeline that synthesizes samples from fashion videos via segmentation and inpainting. Second, the model is fine-tuned on a small, dedicated LVTON dataset to learn the layering logic. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on our LVTON benchmark and demonstrates superior generalizability on traditional VTON benchmarks, setting new state-of-the-art results when fine-tuned and exhibiting zero-shot capabilities.

</details>


### [48] [HALLELUAI: A Hallucination-Aware AI System for Ultra-Realistic Image-to-Video Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22959)
*Aniket Sakpal,Yang Jiang,Rouzbeh Davoudi,Shayan Hassantabar,Mani Najmabadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HALLELUAI是一个端到端的图像到视频生成质量控制系统，通过集成视频审核模块（评估帧级美学、时间运动保真度和幻觉风险）与智能体再生模块（通过提示优化、相机调整、模型/图像切换和重试策略迭代修复失败），实现了符合专家级创意标准的超逼真视频规模化生产。人工评估表明该系统在视觉真实感、品牌安全和图像保真度方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频在营销和创意工作流中应用日益广泛，但缺乏自动化、高精度的质量控制系统，成为规模化生产的瓶颈。

Method: 提出HALLELUAI系统，包含两个核心模块：(1) 视频审核模块——评估帧级美学、时间运动保真度以及与源图像相关的细粒度幻觉风险；(2) 智能体再生模块——通过提示优化、受控相机调整、目标模型或图像切换以及结构化重试策略迭代修复失败案例。审核逻辑与领域特定的创意指南对齐，生成可直接驱动再生的机器可操作反馈。

Result: 在创意专家参与的人工评估中，HALLELUAI表现出与专家标准的高度一致性，能够可靠输出适用于产品和营销场景的超逼真、生产级视频。

Conclusion: 该框架通过强制执行视觉真实感、品牌安全和严格的输入图像保真度，推动了可信AI生成视频内容的发展，实现了图像到视频生成的可规模化应用。

Abstract: AI-generated video is increasingly used across marketing, product storytelling, and creative workflows, yet automated; high-precision quality control remains a major constraint to scaling production. We present HALLELUAI, an end-to-end system that moderates and regenerates image-to-video outputs to meet expert-level creative standards and deliver ultra-realistic videos with consistent end-user quality of experience (QoE) at scale. The system integrates a video moderation module that evaluates frame-level aesthetics, temporal motion fidelity, and fine-grained hallucination risks relative to the source image, with an agentic regeneration module that iteratively fixes failures through prompt refinement, controlled camera adjustments, targeted model or image switching, and structured retry strategies. The moderation logic is aligned with domain-specific creative guidelines and produces granular, machine-actionable feedback that directly drives regeneration. In human-in-the-loop evaluations with creative experts, HALLELUAI shows strong alignment and reliably outputs ultra-realistic, production-grade videos suitable for product and marketing placements at scale. This framework advances trustworthy AI generated video content by enforcing visual realism, brand safety, and strict input-image fidelity while enabling image-to-video generation at scale.

</details>


### [49] [mmSimPrior: Learning Simulation Priors for Data-Efficient Real-World Generalizable Radar-Based Human Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22973)
*Cheng Guo,Qiming Cao,Shengkai Xu,Haoyu Xie,Kaixiang Su,Pu Wang,Hongfei Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: mmSimPrior是一个仿真预训练框架，通过将迁移知识分解为信号、运动和雷达到运动映射三个先验，结合物理信息域随机化课程和多模态信号编码器，在仅需少量真实数据（24个序列）的情况下，将MPJPE降低24.7%-39.0%，并在零样本场景下降低8.5%的MPJPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达在人体运动重建中具有隐私保护和光照鲁棒的优势，但获取多样化的配对雷达-运动数据成本高昂。仿真数据虽然可扩展，但由于多径、杂波、响应统计和分辨率退化等问题，在干净合成信号上训练的模型迁移效果差。

Method: 提出mmSimPrior框架，将迁移知识分解为信号、运动和雷达到运动映射三个先验。使用物理信息域随机化课程预训练多模态信号编码器，模拟传播和采集层面的变化；联合时间分词器学习人类运动的离散先验；共享映射先验支持基于学习运动码本的分类（零样本）和连续回归（少样本自适应）。此外构建了420万帧、3.1万序列的数据集，并引入无重叠评估设置。

Result: 在mmSimPrior-Real和RT-Pose数据集上，仅用24个配对真实序列，mmSimPrior-Reg在三个环境下MPJPE比最强基线降低24.7%-39.0%；mmSimPrior-Cls在无需微调的情况下零样本MPJPE降低8.5%。

Conclusion: 仿真预训练结合知识因子化和物理信息域随机化，能有效弥合仿真与真实毫米波雷达数据之间的鸿沟，在极少量真实数据下实现显著优于基线的人体运动重建性能。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers privacy-preserving and lighting-robust sensing for human motion reconstruction, but learning models that generalize across real deployments require diverse paired radar-motion data that are costly to collect. Simulation provides scalable supervision, yet models trained on clean synthetic signals transfer poorly because of multipath, clutter, response statistics, and resolution degradation. We present mmSimPrior, a simulation-pretrained framework that factorizes transferable knowledge into signal, motion, and radar-to-motion mapping priors. A multi-modal signal encoder is pretrained with a physics-informed domain-randomization curriculum that emulates propagation- and acquisition-level variations, while a joint-temporal tokenizer learns a discrete prior over plausible human motion. A shared mapping prior supports classification over a learned motion codebook for constrained zero-shot reconstruction and continuous regression for flexible limited-data adaptation. We further construct a 4.2M-frame, 31K-sequence dataset suite and introduce a No-Overlap Setting that excludes repeated complete subject-environment-location-motion configurations across adaptation and test. Experiments on mmSimPrior-Real and RT-Pose demonstrate consistent gains: with only 24 paired real sequences, mmSimPrior-Reg reduces MPJPE by 24.7% to 39.0% over the strongest baseline across the three environments, while mmSimPrior-Cls reduces zero-shot MPJPE by 8.5% without finetuning.

</details>


### [50] [Breaking the Synthetic-Real Domain Shortcut for Training-Free Generative Replay-based Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22994)
*Tao Zhang,Qixuan Fan,Yiyuan Liang,Yanjie Wang,Song Yan,Tian Tian,Jiahuan Zhou,Luxin Yan,Sheng Zhong,Xu Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM方法，利用训练免费的文本到图像生成模型合成旧类数据，通过子空间校正、正交投影和真实锚定原型正则化消除域捷径问题，在无样本类增量学习中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式回放在增量训练中直接混合合成旧类数据与真实新类数据会导致严重的性能下降，原因是模型依赖域判别特征（"域捷径"）而非语义类别线索。

Method: DREAM方法包含三个核心组件：（1）使用无需额外训练的冻结文本到图像模型生成旧类数据；（2）通过子空间校正和正交投影消除域捷径；（3）利用真实锚定原型正则化增强语义对齐。

Result: 在4个数据集上的大量实验表明，DREAM超越了现有无样本类增量学习方法，取得了最佳性能。

Conclusion: DREAM通过消除生成数据与真实数据之间的域差异，有效解决了生成式回放中域捷径导致的性能退化问题，为隐私保护场景下的类增量学习提供了有效的无样本解决方案。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) requires models to continuously acquire new knowledge while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. While exemplar replay is effective, it raises concerns regarding privacy and storage. Thus, generative replay has emerged as a viable alternative, synthesizing old data using frozen pretrained text-to-image (T2I) models without any extra training. However, we observe that directly mixing synthetic old-class data with real new-class data during incremental training leads to significant performance degradation. This issue stems from a "domain shortcut", where models rely on domain-discriminative features instead of semantic class cues. To address this, we propose DREAM ($\underline{\mathbf{D}}$omain-$\underline{\mathbf{R}}$egularized $\underline{\mathbf{E}}$xemplar-free $\underline{\mathbf{A}}$lignment $\underline{\mathbf{M}}$odel), which uses a training-free generator to synthesize old-class data and eliminates domain shortcut via subspace rectification and orthogonal projection, while reinforcing semantic alignment through real-anchored prototype regularization. Extensive experiments on 4 datasets demonstrate that DREAM outperforms existing exemplar-free CIL methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/Light-ZhangTao/DREAM.

</details>


### [51] [OmniMate: Open-Ended Real-Time Streaming Audio-Visual Generation for Interactive Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23023)
*Quanyue Song,Yishan He,Yanbo Ding,Zhixiang He,Yongxiang Li,Caigui Jiang,Zhi Zhi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniMate是一个统一的实时交互式音频-视觉虚拟人生成框架，通过生成进度控制器和多参考条件模块，实现了开放式的流式多轮对话生成，在保持长程跨模态身份一致性的同时实现低延迟高质量合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在实时音频驱动虚拟人生成方面取得了进展，但将其扩展到实时交互流式生成仍面临两大挑战：生成范围未知（无法预知对话何时结束）以及长程生成中跨模态身份一致性逐渐退化。

Method: 提出OmniMate框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 生成进度控制器（GPC），显式建模每个流式块的生成进度，使模型能根据期望进度完成响应并在执行与聆听状态间无缝切换；2) 多参考条件模块（MRCM），利用多个参考图像和一个参考语音片段，在长时流式交互中提供持续的视觉和说话人身份线索。

Result: 在交互式改编的VerseBench数据集上，OmniMate实现了高质量、低延迟的流式生成，同时保持了长程音频-视觉一致性。实验表明其支持逼真、连贯、响应的多轮交互式虚拟人体验。

Conclusion: OmniMate有效解决了开放端实时交互式音频-视觉虚拟人生成中的流式进度控制和长程身份保持问题，为真实感多轮对话系统提供了可行的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion-based generative models have enabled real-time audio-driven avatar generation and unified audio-visual synthesis, providing a promising foundation for interactive avatar systems. However, extending these models to real-time interactive streaming remains challenging, as the generation horizon is unknown in advance and cross-modal identity consistency gradually degrades during long-term generation. To address these challenges, we propose OmniMate, a unified framework for open-ended real-time interactive audio-visual avatar generation. OmniMate jointly synthesizes visual content, speech, and audio effects in real time, enabling natural and immersive multi-turn interactions. To achieve adaptive response progression, we introduce a Generation Progress Controller (GPC) that explicitly models the generation progress of each streaming chunk, allowing the model to complete responses according to the desired progress and achieve seamless transitions between execution and listening states. To preserve long-term cross-modal identity consistency, we propose a Multi-Reference Conditioning Module (MRCM), which leverages multiple reference images and a reference speech segment to provide persistent visual and speaker identity cues throughout long-duration streaming interactions. Extensive experiments on an interaction-oriented adaptation of VerseBench demonstrate that OmniMate achieves high-quality, low-latency streaming generation while maintaining strong long-term audio-visual consistency. The results further show that OmniMate supports realistic, coherent, and responsive interactive avatar experiences over extended multi-turn conversations.

</details>


### [52] [When Less Is More: A Controlled Benchmark of Lightweight CNNs for Satellite Land-Cover Segmentation on DeepGlobe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23024)
*Atiq Ur Rehman,Joseph Michael Donovan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在DeepGlobe土地覆盖分类数据集上，系统比较了VGG16、MobileNetV2、InceptionV3、AlexNet和CNN五种架构的精度-效率权衡。在受控条件下，轻量级的MobileNetV2_v1（24.98 MB）以0.7906的总体准确率和0.4625的平均IoU超越了更深的模型，证明了轻量级迁移学习模型在资源受限的遥感环境中可替代复杂深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率卫星影像对土地覆盖分类至关重要，但深度学习语义分割中经典卷积编码器的精度-效率权衡缺乏受控、可重复条件下的量化研究。

Method: 在DeepGlobe数据集上，对VGG16、MobileNetV2、InceptionV3、AlexNet和CNN五种架构进行三个渐进优化迭代比较，使用相同的预处理、超参数和训练协议，无数据增强或类别不平衡校正，以隔离正则化、迁移学习和架构深度的影响。

Result: MobileNetV2_v1以24.98 MB取得最高准确率（0.7906）和平均IoU（0.4625），超越InceptionV3_v2（125.17 MB，准确率0.7610）和VGG16_v2（71.13 MB，准确率0.7653）。在城市、农业和水体类别表现优异，但牧场-裸地分类存在混淆。

Conclusion: 轻量级迁移学习模型在资源受限的遥感环境中可匹配或超越更深模型，实现可扩展的土地覆盖制图，但架构优化无法单独解决光谱相似的少数类混淆问题。

Abstract: High-resolution satellite imagery is the backbone of good land-cover classification, and without that, environmental monitoring, urban planning, and sustainable resource management all fall short. Deep learning architectures perform well in semantic segmentation, but the efficiency-accuracy trade-off across classical convolutional encoders is not well quantified under controlled, reproducible conditions. This study compares five architectures VGG16, MobileNetV2, InceptionV3, AlexNet, and CNN on the DeepGlobe Land Cover Classification dataset using three progressively optimized iterations to isolate regularisation, transfer learning, and architectural depth. To ensure performance differentials reflect architectural properties, all experiments used identical preprocessing, hyperparameter, and training protocols without data augmentation or class-imbalance correction. At 24.98 MB, MobileNetV2_v1 had the highest overall accuracy (0.7906) and mean Intersection over Union (0.4625), outperforming deeper alternatives like InceptionV3_v2 (125.17 MB, accuracy 0.7610) and VGG16_v2 (71.13 MB, accuracy 0.7653). Class-wise analysis showed strength in urban, agricultural, and water categories, but rangeland-barren confusion showed that architectural optimization alone cannot optimize spectrally similar minority classes. Strong spatial generalization and crisp boundary delineation were confirmed on held-out test imagery, validating operational applicability. These results show that lightweight, transfer-learned models can match or outperform deeper models in resource-constrained remote-sensing environments, enabling scalable land-cover mapping.

</details>


### [53] [Structured Redundancy Modeling for Efficient Visual Token Pruning in High-Resolution MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23046)
*Jouwon Song,Woohyeong Kim,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SFPruner提出了一种非迭代的视觉令牌剪枝框架，通过语义引导的岭杠杆机制和基于排名的方向掩码，在单次前向传播中实现冗余感知的重要性选择，显著降低了令牌选择延迟（从112.4ms降至2.5ms），并将理论FLOPs减少转化为实际的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM因生成大量视觉令牌导致严重延迟瓶颈，虽然令牌剪枝可以缓解该问题，但最先进的子集优化方法依赖迭代式子集构建，随着视觉令牌数量增加，顺序依赖导致巨大的选择开销，限制了理论FLOPs缩减向实际加速的转化。

Method: 提出SFPruner，一种非迭代的视觉令牌剪枝框架。包含两个互补机制：(1) 语义引导的岭杠杆机制——在协方差层面抑制冗余，通过整合指令相关性和视觉显著性来抑制主导协方差方向并缓解表示偏差；(2) 基于排名的方向掩码——通过非对称相似性竞争解决残差重叠，高得分令牌通过并行张量操作显式抑制冗余的低得分备选令牌。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL上从512个令牌中选择时，令牌选择过程从112.4ms降至2.5ms，降低了110ms。在激进压缩条件下，保持了与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: SFPruner通过结构性的单次前向传播剪枝框架，成功将理论令牌缩减转化为实际的推理加速，在保持高竞争力的性能同时实现了显著的速度提升。

Abstract: Recent high-resolution Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) generate thousands of visual tokens per input, leading to a visual token explosion that introduces severe latency bottlenecks. While token pruning mitigates this issue, state-of-the-art subset-optimization methods typically rely on iterative subset construction to jointly capture visual diversity and instruction relevance. As visual token counts scale, this sequential dependency introduces significant selection overhead, severely limiting the translation of theoretical FLOPs reductions into actual wall-clock speedups. To address this limitation, we propose Single-Forward Pruner (SFPruner), a structural reformulation of visual token pruning that embeds redundancy control directly into the scoring space, bypassing the need for iterative combinatorial optimization. Our non-iterative framework achieves redundancy-aware importance selection in a single forward pass through two complementary mechanisms. First, to attenuate redundancy at the covariance level, we introduce a semantics-guided ridge leverage scheme. By integrating instruction relevance and visual saliency, this mechanism suppresses dominant covariance directions and mitigates representation bias. Second, ranking-based directional masking resolves residual overlap through asymmetric similarity competition, where higher-scoring tokens explicitly suppress redundant lower-scoring alternatives via parallel tensor operations. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our approach maintains stable selection costs, reducing the token selection process by up to 110 ms, from 112.4 ms to just 2.5 ms at 512 tokens in Qwen2.5-VL. This structural efficiency successfully translates theoretical token reductions into tangible inference speedups while preserving highly competitive performance against state-of-the-art techniques under aggressive compression.

</details>


### [54] [Similarity Is Not Logic: Factored Inference for Dual-Encoder Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23052)
*Sultan Alshehri,Zhantao Yang,Han Zhang,Marios Savvides*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dual-encoder VLM的零样本检索存在逻辑组合约束失效问题（如"雨伞且无人"仍会检索出有人的图片），根源是相似度界面存在"概念袋"效应。本文提出LCSE方法，通过解耦证据提取与约束执行，在不训练的情况下显著提升组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dual-encoder VLM在零样本检索中无法正确执行逻辑组合约束（如AND、NOT），即使单个概念检测可靠，相似度分数仍近似于概念证据的平均池化，忽略操作符语义。

Method: 提出Factored Inference框架，将证据提取与约束执行解耦；具体实现LCSE（Logic-Constrained Score Editing），利用冻结编码器输出的概念分数在外部执行逻辑约束，无需训练。

Result: 在FACTOR-Bench上LCSE达85.5%准确率（最佳微调基线73.2%），SigLIP 2上达90.7%；NegBench COCO MCQ从27.2%提升至65.2%，且检索性能不受损。

Conclusion: VLM组合约束失败的根本瓶颈是相似度聚合方式而非编码器表征能力；外部约束执行是一种有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Dual-encoder vision-language models (VLMs) expose a similarity interface that enables zero-shot retrieval but fails compositional constraints: queries like "umbrella and no person" retrieve images containing both, even when concept detection is reliable. We trace this to an interface-level Bag-of-Concepts effect, where similarity scores approximate mean pooling of concept evidence regardless of operators. Although operator-dependent signals exist in text embeddings, they are too weak or misaligned to affect rankings. Fine-tuning does not reliably resolve this failure because the dominant bottleneck is how similarity aggregates evidence rather than what encoders represent. We propose factored inference, which separates evidence extraction from constraint execution, and introduce LCSE (Logic-Constrained Score Editing), a training-free method that executes constraints externally using concept scores from frozen encoders. We also introduce FACTOR-Bench, where LCSE achieves 85.5% accuracy versus 73.2% for the best fine-tuned baseline, 90.7% when applied to SigLIP 2, and improves NegBench COCO MCQ accuracy from 27.2% to 65.2% while preserving retrieval performance.

</details>


### [55] [DishSeg24k: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Food Segmentation with Stochastic Expert Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23070)
*Yilin Wang,Haochen Shi,Guanyu Chen,Weiqing Min,Jinkai Zheng,Chenggang Yan,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DishSeg24k大规模菜品分割基准数据集（24,096张图像、112,281个实例、278个细粒度类别），并设计FEAST模型，将查询解码建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用强化学习引导的混合专家模块和双评论家解耦优化，在真实餐厅场景中实现最先进的语义分割性能（mIoU +3.21%，mDice +3.68%，mAcc +4.00%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品分割基准无法捕捉真实餐饮场景的复杂性，面临密集菜品间重叠、细粒度类别相似性和极端长尾类别分布等挑战，缺乏大规模、高保真的菜品级分割数据集。

Method: 提出FEAST模型：1) 将基于查询的解码过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），每个解码层更新视为沿菜品边界探索不确定性的顺序决策步骤；2) 重新设计解码器，引入强化学习引导的混合专家（MoE）模块；3) 采用双评论家解耦优化方案，分离任务导向查询优化与结构感知专家路由，防止长尾分布下的专家坍缩。

Result: 在DishSeg24k上，FEAST在mIoU、mDice和mAcc上分别超过先前方法+3.21%、+3.68%和+4.00%，并在FoodSeg103上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: DishSeg24k为真实餐厅场景中的菜品分割提供了高保真基准，FEAST通过MDP建模和RL引导的MoE机制有效解决了密集重叠、细粒度相似和长尾分布等挑战，达到了最先进性能。

Abstract: Food segmentation is essential for applications such as intelligent catering, dietary assessment, and recommendation. However, existing benchmarks fail to capture the complexity of real-world dining scenes. The challenges of dense inter-dish overlap, fine-grained class similarity, and extreme long-tail class distributions exceed the fidelity of current datasets. To fill this gap, we introduce \textbf{DishSeg24k}, a large-scale dish-level segmentation benchmark with 24,096 images, 112,281 instances, and 278 fine-grained categories in real-world dining environments. Based on DishSeg24k, we further propose \textbf{Food Expert-Adaptive Segmentation Transformers (FEAST)} to address these challenges. FEAST models query-based decoding as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), where each decoder layer update is treated as a sequential decision step that explores uncertainty along dish boundaries. We further redesign the decoder with a reinforcement learning (RL)-guided Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) module, in which a dual-critic decoupled optimization scheme separates task-oriented query refinement from structure-aware expert routing. This design promotes expert specialization and prevents expert collapse under long-tail category distributions. Finally, extensive experiments on DishSeg24k demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of FEAST, which outperforms previous methods by {+3.21\%} mIoU, {+3.68\%} mDice, and {+4.00\%} mAcc, respectively. We further validate the effectiveness of FEAST on FoodSeg103. The dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [56] [Inverse Bayesian Inference for Extracting Lesion Dynamics from Longitudinal Spectral CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23078)
*Lukas Förner,Melina Wördehoff,Julian Steffens,Maximilian Schmutz,Rainer Claus,Josua Decker,Thomas Kröncke,Kartikay Tehlan,Thomas Wendler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个逆贝叶斯框架，从纵向光谱CT影像中推断病灶动力学参数，将病灶演化分解为内在动力学、局部环境肿瘤负荷和卫星状态变化三个耦合分量，并在NSCLC数据上验证了区分竞争性与协同性动力学模式的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向医学影像能够捕捉病灶的时间演化，但从中提取控制演化的动力学参数仍然具有挑战性。现有方法多停留在静态特征提取，缺乏对病灶行为的机制性刻画。

Method: 提出逆贝叶斯推断框架，将光谱特征演化分解为三项：内在动力学（A_i）、局部环境肿瘤负荷（B·n，通过卫星计数耦合）和卫星状态变化（C·Δx_sat）。在光子计数CT的NSCLC转移瘤数据上验证，并进行合成数据验证和交叉耦合分析。

Result: 肺病灶表现出显著的卫星计数耦合（B=-0.34, p<0.05），提示竞争性动力学；肝病灶表现出协同性卫星行为耦合（C≈+1.0, p<0.05）。合成数据验证了参数恢复能力，交叉耦合分析确认了非零耦合的检测能力。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了逆动力学推断作为一种从纵向影像中提取可解释参数的原理性方法，推动从静态特征提取向病灶行为的机制性刻画迈进。

Abstract: Longitudinal medical imaging captures temporal evolution of lesions, yet extracting the underlying dynamical parameters governing this evolution remains challenging. We propose an inverse Bayesian framework for inferring lesion dynamics from longitudinal spectral CT. We decompose spectral feature ($x$) evolution into three components: \begin{equation*}
  \frac{dx_i}{dt} = A_i x_i + B \cdot n + C \cdot Δx_{\text{sat}} \end{equation*} where $A_i$ captures intrinsic dynamics (lesion-autonomous evolution), $B$ captures local environment tumour burden (organ tumour burden through satellite count coupling), and $C$ captures environment/satellite state change (i.e., whether surrounding lesions move similarly or not).
  We demonstrate the framework on photon-counting NSCLC CT data from metastases, recovering distinct dynamical regimes: lung lesions exhibit significant satellite count coupling ($B=-0.34$, $p<0.05$) suggesting competitive dynamics, while liver lesions show synergistic satellite behaviour coupling ($C\approx+1.0$, $p<0.05$). Synthetic validation confirms parameter recovery, and cross-coupling analysis validates that our method detects non-zero coupling when present.
  This work establishes inverse dynamical inference as a principled methodology for extracting interpretable parameters from longitudinal imaging, moving beyond static feature extraction toward mechanistic characterisation of lesion behaviour. The code and data are available at: https://github.com/lukasf98/inverse-bayesian-inference

</details>


### [57] [SHReg: Strictly Rotation-Equivariant Point Cloud Registration via Spherical Harmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23096)
*Chongjian Wang,Junjie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SHReg，一个基于SO(3)表示理论的严格旋转等变点云配准框架。通过将局部几何特征表示为SO(3)的不可约表示，SHReg无需局部参考帧即可保证任意旋转下的精确等变性，并联合学习旋转不变描述子和旋转等变特征，使每个对应点可直接假设刚体变换，减少对RANSAC的依赖，在多个基准上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的点云配准方法通常依赖局部参考帧或数据增强来近似旋转不变性，仅提供经验层面的不变性，在未知旋转变换下性能显著下降。为此，作者旨在构建严格旋转等变的配准框架，从理论上保证对任意旋转的精确等变性。

Method: 基于SO(3)表示理论，将局部几何特征表示为SO(3)的不可约表示，构建基于球谐函数的等变骨干网络；联合学习旋转不变描述子（用于鲁棒匹配）和旋转等变特征（保留细粒度方向信息）；利用保留的等变结构，每个对应点可直接假设刚体变换，减少RANSAC采样依赖。

Result: 在3DMatch、3DLoMatch和KITTI数据集上的实验表明，SHReg在配准精度上持续超越现有最优方法，尤其是在大旋转扰动场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 严格旋转等变性为点云配准提供了理论保障，能够在不依赖局部参考帧或数据增强的情况下，有效应对任意旋转变换，同时通过等变结构降低了对传统RANSAC假设采样的需求，提升了鲁棒性。

Abstract: Point cloud registration critically depends on local features that are both distinctive and robust to arbitrary 3D rotations. Existing learning-based methods typically approximate rotation invariance via fragile local reference frames or extensive data augmentation, providing only empirical invariance and often degrading under unseen rotational transformations. In this paper, we propose SHReg, a strictly rotation-equivariant point cloud registration framework grounded in the representation theory of $SO(3)$. By representing local geometric features as irreducible representations of $SO(3)$, SHReg guarantees exact equivariance under arbitrary rotations without relying on local reference frames. Built upon a spherical-harmonics-based equivariant backbone, SHReg jointly learns rotation-invariant descriptors for robust correspondence matching and rotation-equivariant features that preserve fine-grained orientation information. The preserved equivariant structure enables each correspondence to directly hypothesize a rigid transformation, reducing reliance on large-scale hypothesis sampling in conventional RANSAC-based pipelines and leading to improved robustness under challenging rotational variations. Extensive experiments on 3DMatch, 3DLoMatch, and KITTI demonstrate that SHReg consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in registration accuracy, particularly under large rotational perturbations.

</details>


### [58] [DispatchRAG: Grounding Emergency Dispatch Decisions in Real-World Protocols from Traffic Accident Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23132)
*Muhammad Sulthan Adhipradhana,Ehsan Javanmardi,Naren Bao,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DispatchRAG框架，结合RAG检索机制和LLM推理器，基于日本真实交通事故响应协议，增强视觉语言模型在交通事故严重性评估和应急响应中的能力，并引入Accident Dispatch Dataset进行验证，结果表明优于基线VLM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故严重性评估对决定派遣何种紧急服务至关重要，但现有VLM缺乏真实事故响应协议支撑，无法直接用于事故严重性评估。

Method: 提出DispatchRAG框架，包含基于RAG的检索机制获取最相关事故协议，以及基于LLM的推理器生成最合适的应急响应方案。

Result: 在Accident Dispatch Dataset上验证，DispatchRAG在各种事故场景下的表现优于基线VLM模型。

Conclusion: DispatchRAG可有效增强VLM在真实事故场景中的应急响应能力，有望集成到自动驾驶车辆中实现自主事故报告和响应。

Abstract: Assessing the severity of a traffic accident scenario is important to decide which emergency service to dispatch. Missing an ambulance dispatch on a pedestrian accident is a fatal issue that can lead to death. Recently, Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been a promising tool for accident reasoning, yet many VLMs are not grounded in real-life accident response protocols, making them not usable in accident severity assessment off-the-shelf. We introduced DispatchRAG, an accident assessor and dispatcher framework grounded in real-life Japanese traffic-accident response protocols, designed to enhance VLMs to generate an appropriate emergency response during an emergency scenario. Utilizing a RAG-based retrieval mechanism to retrieve the most relevant accident protocol and an LLM-powered reasoner to suggest the most proper response. To support evaluation, we introduce Accident Dispatch Dataset, a comprehensive dataset of accident assessment and emergency response according to Japanese accident response protocols adapted from the MM-AU dataset. We validate our framework on the Accident Dispatch Dataset, showing strong performance across various accident scenarios compared to the baseline VLM, pointing toward integration in autonomous vehicles that can automatically report both their own and nearby accidents.

</details>


### [59] [Towards Dual-Brain Minimal Sufficient Representation for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23181)
*Yihao Wu,Chenyi Xu,Liqi Yan,Chenhuan Cai,Geyong Min,Bin Lin,Fangli Guan,Jianhui Zhang,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainNav提出基于最小充分性原则的VLN-CE框架，通过逻辑锚定模型、极简约束对齐模块和压缩世界模型三个组件，在保持语义与空间对齐的同时过滤冗余信息，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法中，多模态大模型和基于世界模型的方法保留了过多任务无关细节，导致泛化能力下降和计算负担增加。作者希望构建一个遵循最小充分性原则的导航框架，在保留必要信息的同时抑制环境噪声和冗余信息。

Method: BrainNav包含三个核心模块：(1) 逻辑锚定模型（Logical Anchor Model）：实现指令感知的选择性感知，抑制环境噪声；(2) 极简约束对齐模块（Minimalist Constraint Alignment）：作为紧凑的跨模态瓶颈，同步离散语言意图与连续潜动态，过滤冗余信息；(3) 压缩世界模型（Compression World Model）：在压缩的低秩潜空间中预测动作条件状态。

Result: 在R2R-CE val-unseen上，SR/SPL分别提升2.0%/1.0；在RxR-CE val-unseen上，SR/SPL分别提升0.94%/0.78，均超越先前SOTA。

Conclusion: 最小充分的世界表征为鲁棒的视觉-语言导航提供了有效基础，验证了在复杂导航任务中精简信息、聚焦关键语义的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to ground language in egocentric observations and plan in unseen scenes. Although recent multimodal large models and world-model-based methods have improved navigation, they often preserve excessive task-irrelevant detail, weakening generalization and increasing computational burden. We propose BrainNav, a navigation framework grounded in the Principle of Minimal Sufficiency. BrainNav consists of three components: a Logical Anchor Model that implements instruction-aware selective perception to suppress environmental noise, a Minimalist Constraint Alignment module that serves as a compact cross-modal bottleneck, efficiently synchronizing discrete linguistic intent with continuous latent dynamics while filtering out redundant information, and a Compression World Model that predicts action-conditioned states within a condensed, low-rank latent space. These modules align semantic intent with spatial perception, enhancing the agent's robustness and efficiency in complex tasks. Experiments show that BrainNav improves over prior SOTA by 2.0 % / 1.0 in SR/SPL on R2R-CE val-unseen and 0.94 % / 0.78 on RxR-CE val-unseen. These results indicate that minimally sufficient world representations provide an effective foundation for robust VLN.

</details>


### [60] [Fashion-3DLR: A Controllable 3D Garment Generation Using Pairwise Fashion Elements for Intelligent Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23189)
*Shenghao Yang,Hongtao Zhang,Yuhan Yi,Zhihao Tang,Zihao Cui,Lian Wen,Han Yan,Yuan Gao,Mingbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Fashion-3DLR框架，通过Garment Feature Fusion Diffusion Transformer (GFF-DiT)模块将2D服装设计元素（草图、纹理）融合到潜空间，再利用整流流变压器生成几何潜码，解码为3D高斯和网格等3D服装表示，支持物理仿真和虚拟试穿，性能超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D生成模型已成熟，但3D服装生成仍处早期阶段，时尚设计中不同设计元素的语义信息在3D表示中存在复杂的耦合关系，给生成多样化的3D服装带来巨大挑战。

Method: 提出Fashion-3DLR框架，包含：1) Garment Feature Fusion Diffusion Transformer (GFF-DiT)模块，用于将2D时尚设计元素（如草图和纹理）融合到潜空间；2) 在潜空间中使用整流流变压器生成几何潜码；3) 将潜码解码为多种3D服装表示（3D高斯和网格）。

Result: Fashion-3DLR超越了之前的SOTA方法，能够生成结构良好、非水密的服装，并支持物理仿真和虚拟试穿。

Conclusion: Fashion-3DLR可作为多功能3D服装设计工具，在下游任务（3D高斯泼溅驱动的布料物理仿真和基于网格的虚拟试穿）中展现出潜力。

Abstract: AI-generated content (AIGC) has made significant progress, with 2D generative models becoming ready-to-use tools for the digital fashion industry. However, 3D garment generation remains in its nascent stage, where in the realm of fashion, the semantic information of diverse design elements exhibits intricate coupling relationships in 3D representations, posing substantial challenges for generating diverse 3D garments. In this work, to handle the above problem, We introduce Fashion-3DLR, a novel 3D garment generation framework that utilizes diverse design elements to create high-quality, versatile 3D garment assets. Specifically, to bridge the semantic gaps between different fashion elements, we propose a Garment Feature Fusion Diffusion Transformer (GFF-DiT) module to integrate 2D fashion design elements, e.g., sketch and texture, into latent space. Within the latent space, we then employ a rectified flow transformer to generate geometry latents, which can be decoded into various 3D garment representations, including 3D Gaussians and meshes. Furthermore, we integrate Fashion-3DLR into downstream tasks, achieving the 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)-driven cloth physical simulation and mesh-based virtual try-on. Experimental results indicate that Fashion-3DLR surpass the previous state-of-the-art methods, which verify that the proposed work can generate well-structured, non-watertight garments capable of physical simulation and virtual try-on, underscoring its potential as a versatile 3D garment design tool.

</details>


### [61] [OmniScope: Modality-Decoupled Token Compression for Omnimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23193)
*Jinsen Su,Yongdong Luo,Yuexiao Ma,Yibo Hu,Meiguang Jin,Xiaowu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有全模态大语言模型的token压缩方法通常依赖单一模态指导另一模态的保留内容，但跨模态显著性不匹配会导致关键信息丢失。本文提出OmniScope，一种免训练token压缩框架，以查询为共享语义锚点，分别估计音频和视频的显著性，采用锚点-差分策略裁剪视觉token、按秒合并音频token。在四个音视频基准和两个模型规模上，OmniScope在25%保留率下实现3.53倍预填充加速、15%以上显存降低，准确率仅下降0.35个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大语言模型的token压缩方法多采用跨模态单向指导（如用视觉显著性指导音频压缩或反之），但这种假设存在问题：同一查询下，音频和视频的显著时刻往往不同步（cross-modal salience mismatch），导致单向指导在激进压缩下丢弃回答关键信息。

Method: OmniScope是一个免训练的token压缩框架，核心思路为"共享查询但不共享显著性估计"：1）以查询（query）作为共享语义锚点，分别独立估计音频和视频模态的显著性；2）为不同模态分配独立的token预算；3）视觉token采用锚点-差分（anchor-delta）策略，保留全局上下文同时捕捉时序变化；4）音频token按秒合并以减少冗余同时维持时序连续性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni两个模型规模下，覆盖四个音视频基准测试，OmniScope在所有压缩设置下均取得最佳平均准确率。在25%整体token保留率下，预填充速度提升3.53倍，GPU显存降低超过15%，平均准确率仅下降0.35个百分点。

Conclusion: 全模态大语言模型推理的一个简洁设计原则：共享查询（query）但不共享显著性估计（salience estimates）。

Abstract: Existing token compression methods for omnimodal large language models typically rely on one modality to determine what to retain in the other. We show that this assumption often breaks down: for the same query, audio and video relevance often peaks at different moments. This cross-modal salience mismatch makes unidirectional guidance prone to discarding answer-critical cues under aggressive compression. We propose OmniScope, a training-free token compression framework that uses the query as a shared semantic anchor while estimating relevance separately for audio and video. OmniScope allocates modality-specific token budgets, prunes visual tokens with an anchor-delta strategy that preserves both global context and temporal changes, and merges audio tokens within each second to reduce redundancy while maintaining temporal continuity. Across four audio-video benchmarks and two Qwen2.5-Omni model scales, OmniScope achieves the best average accuracy across all compression settings. At 25% overall token retention, it delivers up to 3.53x prefill speedup and more than 15% GPU memory reduction, with only a 0.35-point drop in average accuracy. These results suggest a simple design principle for OmniLLM inference: share the query across modalities, but not the salience estimates. The code is available at https://github.com/MAC-AutoML/OmniScope.

</details>


### [62] [Out-of-Length Scene Text Recognition: A Two-Axis Diagnosis and a Training-Free Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23194)
*Zobeir Raisi,John Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文诊断并解决了场景文字识别模型在长文本上的性能失败问题。通过分析发现编码器宽度（而非解码器长度）是主导故障模式，并提出一种无需修改模型权重的推理时切片-拼接方法：将长图像按训练宽度切分成重叠块，独立解码后通过几何锚定的编辑距离对齐进行拼接。该方法在LTB基准上达到42.79-47.11%的词准确率，匹配或超越SOTA，且与普通解码速度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STR模型几乎仅在最多25字符的词裁剪图像上训练，但实际部署（如标牌、产品标签、密集字幕）需要读取更长的文本。本文旨在诊断长文本读取失败的根本原因并加以解决。

Method: 将长文本失败解耦为编码器宽度轴和解码器时间轴两个外推维度，诊断发现编码器宽度是主导故障。尝试了无训练旋转缩放（恢复2-4点CER）和加权微调（恢复6-8点CER）等表征侧修复，但仍存在解码机制导致的残余差距。最终提出推理时切片策略：将长图像按训练宽度切分为重叠块，独立解码后通过几何锚定的编辑距离对齐拼接。

Result: 该方法在两个基座检查点上达到42.79-43.05%的桶平均词准确率，匹配SOTA（41.57%），在最困难桶上高出11-12点；应用于PARSeq检查点达到47.11%。解码速度与普通解码相当。应用切片后微调不再带来收益。

Conclusion: 编码器宽度是STR长文本失败的主要因素，解码侧修复（推理时切片-拼接）足以匹配甚至超越专用架构的性能，无需修改模型权重。

Abstract: Scene Text Recognition (STR) models are trained almost exclusively on word crops of at most 25 characters, yet real deployments (signage, product labels, dense captions) require reading much longer text. This paper diagnoses that failure and then closes it. The diagnosis separates out-of-length failure into two simultaneously extrapolating axes (the encoder's width axis and the decoder's time axis) and shows that encoder width, not decoder length, is the dominant failure mode. Representation-side fixes bring only partial relief: training-free rotary rescalings recover at most 2-4 points of character error rate (CER), and a weighted fine-tuning recipe recovers 6-8 points while improving standard-benchmark accuracy, yet word accuracy on the Long Text Benchmark (LTB) stays near zero, because the residual gap lies in the decoding mechanism rather than the representation. We then close that gap at inference time, on an unmodified word-level checkpoint: the long image is sliced into overlapping crops at the model's training width, each decoded independently and in-distribution, and the reads stitched by geometry-anchored edit-distance alignment. This procedure reaches 42.79-43.05% bucket-average word accuracy on LTB across two base checkpoints, matching the published state of the art (41.57%) and beating it by 11-12 points on the hardest bucket, at wall-clock parity with plain decoding; applied unchanged to the public PARSeq checkpoint it reaches 47.11%. Once chunking is applied fine-tuning no longer helps: the decoding-side fix alone matches purpose-built architectures. We release the diagnosis harness and implementation.

</details>


### [63] [Counterfactual Motion Reliability Learning for Robust UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23209)
*Yuehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CMRTrack，一种反事实运动可靠性学习框架，通过区分目标一致运动与背景伪运动，实现红外无人机鲁棒跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外无人机跟踪中目标小、低对比度、易与热干扰或杂乱背景混淆；现有Transformer跟踪器在目标外观弱时响应易被背景结构主导；引入运动线索虽自然但不可靠（相机抖动、动态背景、传感器噪声、目标消失等产生比真实目标运动更强的时序变化），关键挑战在于如何区分目标一致运动与背景诱导的伪运动。

Method: 1) 轻量运动证据编码器从相邻搜索区域提取时序证据；2) 训练时引入反事实目标擦除历史分支构建硬运动参考，促使运动编码器学习可靠的目标一致运动；3) 通过运动引导令牌调制和可靠性感知分数融合，将所学运动证据融入单流跟踪框架，实现自适应特征增强和响应精炼。

Result: 在Anti-UAV410数据集上持续优于代表性SOTA跟踪器，显著提升OSTrack基线性能；消融研究和定性分析验证了反事实运动可靠性学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 反事实运动可靠性学习能有效区分目标一致运动与背景伪运动，是提升红外无人机跟踪鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Infrared unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) tracking is challenging because the target is often small, low-contrast, and easily confused with thermal distractors or cluttered backgrounds. Recent Transformer-based trackers have achieved promising performance by learning strong appearance representations, but their responses can still be dominated by background structures when the target appearance is weak or ambiguous. A natural solution is to introduce temporal motion cues. However, in infrared UAV tracking, motion cues are not always reliable: camera jitter, dynamic backgrounds, sensor noise, and target disappearance may produce temporal variations that are stronger than the true target motion. Therefore, the key challenge is not simply how to use motion, but how to distinguish target-consistent motion from background-induced pseudo motion. To this end, we propose CMRTrack, a counterfactual motion reliability learning framework for robust infrared UAV tracking. CMRTrack first extracts temporal evidence from adjacent search regions using a lightweight motion evidence encoder. During training, a counterfactual target-erased history branch is introduced to construct hard motion references, encouraging the motion encoder to learn reliable target-consistent motion rather than arbitrary temporal changes. The learned motion evidence is then incorporated into a one-stream tracking framework through motion-guided token modulation and reliability-aware score fusion, enabling adaptive feature enhancement and response refinement. Extensive experiments on Anti-UAV410 demonstrate that CMRTrack consistently outperforms representative state-of-the-art trackers and significantly improves the OSTrack baseline, with ablation studies and qualitative analysis verifying the effectiveness of the proposed counterfactual motion reliability learning.

</details>


### [64] [BoneAgeTW2: Automated Skeletal Maturation Assessment via the Tanner-Whitehouse 2 Method, Deep Learning, and Clinical Report Generation with Distribution Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23224)
*Juan Manuel Castillo Pinto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoneAgeTW2 是首个完全开源的端到端 Tanner-Whitehouse 2 骨骼成熟度评估系统，采用 YOLOv8 检测 20 块手骨，EfficientNet-B3 多分类头同步评估成熟阶段，并自动生成含高斯分布曲线的临床 PDF 报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨骼成熟度评估方法缺乏完全开源、端到端自动化且严格遵循 TW2 临床协议的系统，临床实践中依赖人工评估，耗时且主观性强。

Method: 使用 YOLOv8 从 X 光片中精确检测和定位 20 块 TW2 手骨；采用 EfficientNet-B3 作为骨干网络，搭配 20 个独立分类头同时对各骨骼进行成熟阶段（A-I）分类；利用公开 RSNA 数据集（12,611 张手部 X 光片），通过伪标签策略从全局骨龄标注中推导出每块骨骼的阶段标签。

Result: 构建了首个完全开源的端到端 TW2 骨骼成熟度评估系统，实现了 20 块手骨的同步检测与成熟阶段分类，并自动生成包含交互式高斯分布曲线的临床 PDF 报告。

Conclusion: BoneAgeTW2 提供了完全开源、可复现的 TW2 协议自动化解决方案，有助于推动骨骼成熟度评估的标准化和临床普及。

Abstract: We present BoneAgeTW2, the first fully open-source system to automate the complete Tanner-Whitehouse 2 (TW2) clinical protocol for skeletal maturity assessment end-to-end. The system employs YOLOv8 for precise detection and localization of the 20 TW2 hand bones from radiographic images, and an EfficientNet-B3 backbone with 20 independent classification heads to assign maturation stages (A-I) to each bone simultaneously. From these predictions, the system automatically generates clinical PDF reports including interactive Gaussian distribution curves for all 20 bones, enabling direct comparison with population norms. The model is trained on the public RSNA Pediatric Bone Age Challenge dataset (12,611 hand radiographs) using a pseudo-labeling strategy to derive per-bone stage labels from global bone age annotations. The full codebase is publicly available at https://github.com/jmmana/BoneAgeTW2.

</details>


### [65] [A Reconstruction-Based Framework for Caption Evaluation Beyond Reference Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23235)
*Zhijiang Tang,Jiaxin Qi,Kaihua Tang,Yuhua Zheng,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重建原则的无参考图像描述评估方法：一个描述的好坏取决于其能否重建出与原图语义等价的图像，并通过下游视觉-语言任务来评估语义等价性，同时引入了低成本的CTTD替代数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像描述评估依赖人工标注的参考描述，但参考描述的内容受标注者意图和描述能力影响，无法客观衡量描述对图像语义的保真度。因此需要一种不依赖参考描述、直接评估描述与图像语义一致性的方法。

Method: 提出重建原则：描述的质量由其重建原图的能力决定，但重建目标不是像素级匹配，而是语义等价。通过下游视觉-语言任务（如VQA、检索等）来检验重建图像与原图是否语义等价，从而得到无参考、任务条件下的描述评分。同时引入低成本的图灵测试数据集（CTTD）作为替代方案。

Result: 分析了各组件依赖的局限性，并证明了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于语义等价重建的无参考评估框架是可行的，能够更客观地衡量描述对图像语义的保真度，CTTD可降低计算成本。

Abstract: Image captioning is a primary task in vision--language research, yet assessing how faithfully a caption preserves image semantics without relying on reference captions remains unsettled. Prevailing evaluations rely on human-annotated references, whose content reflects annotator intent and captioning proficiency. In this paper, we study a reconstruction-based principle for caption evaluation: a caption is as good as its capacity to enable reconstruction of the original image. However, because captioning inherently compresses visual information, it is impossible to recover all details, and pixel-wise comparison between reconstructed and source images is neither feasible nor meaningful. Through our in-depth analysis of the nature of captions, whose fundamental purpose is to transmit the semantic content of an image, we propose a revised principle: a caption is as good as its capacity to enable a reconstruction that is semantically equivalent to the original. To assess semantic equivalence, we test whether the reconstruction matches the original image across a suite of downstream vision--language tasks, yielding a reference-free, task-conditioned caption score. We characterize component-dependent limitations and introduce the lower-cost Captioning Turing Test Dataset (CTTD) surrogate.

</details>


### [66] [SARATR-X-v2: Scale-Aware Structural Pre-Training for SAR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23238)
*Weijie Li,Yafei Song,Yongxiang Liu,Bowen Peng,Jie Zhou,Jingyuan Xia,Wei Yang,Tianpeng Liu,Zhen Liu,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAR预训练重建目标应满足物理基础稳定性和语义尺度兼容性两个条件，并设计了SARATR-X-v2，通过固定结构提取器与可学习权重融合的统一监督信号，在12个SAR基准上实现最先进迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码图像建模的预训练重建目标设计尚未达成共识，针对SAR图像相干成像中乘性散斑噪声及下游任务多尺度需求，亟需一个兼具物理稳定性和语义尺度兼容性的预训练目标设计框架。

Method: 设计SARATR-X-v2：通过固定结构提取器（覆盖6种感受野，从盲点局部聚合到方向对数比区域对比）提取结构特征，并通过可学习权重融合为统一的监督信号，用于掩码重建预训练。

Result: 在12个SAR基准（涵盖分类、检测、分割）上达到SOTA迁移性能；在合成散斑扰动下，表示扰动漂移较像素空间监督降低近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 有效的SAR预训练不在于重建更多信号，而在于重建正确的结构目标；物理基础稳定性和语义尺度兼容性为相干成像下的预训练目标设计提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Masked image modeling has become a dominant paradigm for SAR pre-training, yet the design of the reconstruction target remains fundamentally unsettled. This article argues that a SAR pre-training target should satisfy two conditions to produce transferable representations: (i) physics-grounded stability, i.e., approximate invariance of the target operator to multiplicative speckle inherent in coherent imaging; and (ii) semantic scale compatibility, i.e., coverage of the heterogeneous spatial scales that downstream tasks demand. These two conditions are individually achievable but jointly difficult: physics-grounded stability favors fixed operators, while semantic scale compatibility favors data-driven composition. To this end, SARATR-X-v2 reconciles both within a single design. The target is constructed through fixed structural extractors spanning six receptive fields, from blind-spot local aggregation to directional log-ratio region contrast, and fused via learnable weights into one unified supervision signal for masked reconstruction. On twelve SAR benchmarks across classification, detection, and segmentation, SARATR-X-v2 achieves state-of-the-art transfer performance. Under synthetic speckle variation, the proposed target reduces perturbation drift in the learned representation by nearly two orders of magnitude relative to pixel-space supervision. Taken together, these results establish physics-grounded stability and semantic scale compatibility as a principled framework for pre-training target design under coherent imaging, and suggest that effective SAR pre-training is not about reconstructing more signal, but about reconstructing the right structural target.

</details>


### [67] [WaveZip: Wavelet-Driven Space-Time Decoupling for Video Token Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23265)
*Yuhui Zeng,Wang Chen,Jinfa Huang,Tianyu Xie,Yongdong Luo,Jiayi Ji,Xiawu Zheng,jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveZip提出了一种联合信号-频率域的框架，利用离散小波变换（DWT）解耦时空冗余，无需训练即可高效压缩视觉token，在10倍压缩比下保持99.6%的完整性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在处理长视频理解时面临视觉token二次计算开销的问题。现有的高效方法通过硬剪枝或均匀合并进行token压缩，但这些方法仅在空间特征域操作，导致鲁棒的结构上下文和判别性语义细节相互纠缠，难以有效分离冗余和关键信息。

Method: WaveZip采用离散小波变换（DWT）在时域和空域联合操作。时域上，利用1D DWT分析查询帧相关性，高频系数进一步通过帧间差异门控，共同驱动帧级token预算的动态分配。空域上，2D DWT将特征分解为低频近似和高频细节，高频系数在查询显著区域内调制以调控空间重建。该方法无需任务特定训练，可无缝集成到现有LVLMs中。

Result: 在长视频理解基准测试中，WaveZip在10倍极端压缩比下保留了99.6%的完整性能，持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: WaveZip通过信号-频率域联合框架有效解耦了时空冗余和显著信息，实现了高效且无损的长视频推理，且无需额外训练即可即插即用。

Abstract: Existing Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) struggle with long-form video understanding due to the quadratic computational cost of visual tokens. While recent efficient methods attempt to compress tokens via hard pruning or uniform merging, they operate strictly in the spatial feature domain, where robust structural context and discriminative semantic details are inherently entangled. In this work, we propose WaveZip, a joint signal-frequency-domain framework for efficient video inference. Driven by the insight that temporal redundancy resides in low-pass approximation scales while spatial saliency strongly correlates with high-frequency components, WaveZip leverages Discrete Wavelet Transforms (DWT) to disentangle these signals. Temporally, it employs 1D DWT to analyze query-frame relevance, and the resulting high-frequency coefficients are further gated by inter-frame differences, with both signals jointly driving the dynamic allocation of a precise frame-level token budget. Spatially, a 2D DWT decomposes features into low-frequency approximations and high-frequency detail components, where the high-frequency coefficients are modulated within query-salient regions to regulate spatial reconstruction. Importantly, WaveZip requires no task-specific training and can be seamlessly integrated into off-the-shelf LVLMs to boost inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on long video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that WaveZip retains 99.6% of the full performance under an extreme 10x compression ratio, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [68] [What CLIP Knows but Cannot Say: Recovering Negation from Frozen Intermediate Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23271)
*Chen-Yi Lu,Yueh-Shao Chen,Somali Chaterji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIP等对比性视觉-语言模型由于"表征坍缩"现象（最终层丢失了中间层构建的句法结构），对否定词不敏感。本文提出PeakPatch，一种轻量级后置校正系统，通过在编码器句法峰值层截取信号，使用嵌入校正网络（ECN）和分数校正网络（SCN）恢复否定信息，仅增加5.2M参数（3.5%），在NegBench上显著超越现有方法（COCO MCQ +35.1），且无需修改预训练权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等模型将语义相反的短语（如"a dog" vs. "not a dog"）编码为几乎相同的嵌入，对否定不敏感。作者发现这是由于文本编码器最终层在提升视觉对齐过程中坍塌了中间层构建的句法结构，称为"表征坍缩"。

Method: 提出PeakPatch系统：1) 找到CLIP文本编码器中句法组合性最强的中间层（句法峰值层）；2) 嵌入校正网络（ECN）通过交叉注意力从峰值层提取否定特定信号，预测偏差向量注入最终层嵌入空间；3) 分数校正网络（SCN）预测有界标量分数偏移用于判别任务。所有CLIP参数冻结，仅训练新增的5.2M参数。

Result: 在NegBench上：COCO MCQ达74.3%（超过CLIP基线35.1，超过最优编码器微调方法17.8），VOC MCQ达65.5%。在完全分布外否定检索任务上超越所有微调基线。校正后的嵌入可迁移至文生图任务（否定分数提升18.4），且在ViT-B/32、ViT-L/14、SigLIP等多种骨干上泛化。

Conclusion: PeakPatch通过轻量级后置校正有效恢复了CLIP模型丢失的否定信号，揭示了表征坍缩现象，提供了一种无需修改预训练权重的解决方案，在多个任务和骨干网络上展现出良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP map semantically opposite phrases (e.g., "a dog" vs. "not a dog") to nearly identical embeddings, rendering them insensitive to negation. We attribute this failure to a phenomenon we call Representational Collapse: by tracking compositional divergence and visual alignment across the CLIP text encoder, we show that middle layers build compositional syntax, but the final layers collapse this structure as visual alignment rises, producing a syntax-blind final representation. To recover the lost negation signal without altering pretrained weights, we propose PeakPatch, a lightweight post-hoc correction system that intercepts the encoder at its compositional peak. An Embedding Correction Network (ECN) uses cross-attention to extract a negation-specific signal from the peak layer, anchored to a stable baseline, and predicts a deviation vector that re-injects the lost syntax into the final-layer embedding space. A complementary Score Correction Network (SCN) predicts bounded scalar score offsets for discriminative tasks. Both modules are trained jointly end-to-end while all CLIP parameters remain frozen, adding only 5.2M parameters (3.5% of the backbone) and preserving the standard cosine similarity interface. On NegBench, PeakPatch achieves 74.3% on COCO MCQ (+35.1 over CLIP, +17.8 over the best encoder fine-tuning method) and 65.5% on VOC MCQ, while outperforming all fine-tuning baselines on fully out-of-distribution negation retrieval despite training only 3.5% of the parameters. The corrected embeddings also transfer to text-to-image generation (+18.4 negation score) and generalize across ViT-B/32, ViT-L/14, and SigLIP backbones. Project URL: https://stevencylu.github.io/PeakPatch/.

</details>


### [69] [The Gate Always Closes: On Injecting Auxiliary Signals into Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23335)
*Moshiur Farazi,Sameera Ramasinghe,Bekir Sait Ciftler,Mahbub Ahmed Turza,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLMs中可学习门控通路会被优化器自动关闭（零信号），即使门参数名义上通过30-45%的信号，这些通路在行为上也是关闭的。作者将这一抑制现象归因于死梯度区和负效用区，并转而利用该现象：通过在训练时加入双曲视觉关系图的几何辅助损失（IoA驱动的蕴含锥和洛伦兹流形上的角度排斥），推理时丢弃，对LoRA微调进行正则化。实验表明，训练时正则化单独无法保护关系准确率，必须保留几何推理通路；几何通路在分布外（VSR）场景中对于保留空间信号至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现VLMs中可学习门控通路被优化器自动关闭（零信号）的普遍现象，并探究其背后的死梯度区和负效用区机制。作者不试图对抗这种抑制，而是利用它来正则化LoRA微调，通过在训练时引入双曲视觉关系图的几何辅助损失，在推理时丢弃，以提升模型在关系推理任务上的表现。

Method: 1. 分析了五种注射设计中可学习门控通路的行为，发现门参数名义上通过30-45%信号但精度不变。2. 归因于两个机制：死梯度区（基于图像信号的标题不变性）和负效用区（辅助信号损害损失）。3. 提出利用抑制现象：在LoRA微调训练时加入双曲视觉关系图的几何辅助损失（IoA驱动的蕴含锥和洛伦兹流形上的角度排斥），推理时丢弃这些几何损失。4. 消融实验：比较有无几何损失、是否在推理时保留几何通路的不同配置。5. 在GQA（按问题类型分解）和VSR（分布外）上评估。

Result: 1. 无几何损失的三种配置在关系问题上损失2.85-3.39pp，在属性问题上增益~1pp。2. 有训练时几何损失但推理时用软提示的配置在关系上损失5.14pp，属性仅+0.23pp，说明训练时正则化不能保护关系准确率。3. 保留几何推理通路的配置保持了原关系准确率并匹配属性增益。4. 在VSR上，RMS-prefix配方保留空间信号；去除几何损失（G2）使VSR下降4.6pp，隔离出OOD来源。5. 次要结果：嵌入范数对齐对生成安全的prefix注入是必要的，可学习门控应替换为固定非可选注射。

Conclusion: VLMs中可学习门控通路普遍被抑制（关闭），不应试图对抗这一现象，而应利用它。提出了一种有效的正则化方法：在训练时加入双曲几何辅助损失，推理时丢弃，能够保护关系推理准确率的同时提升属性推理性能。保留几何推理通路是必要的，训练时正则化单独不足以保护关系准确率。同时建议使用固定非可选注射替代可学习门控。

Abstract: Auxiliary signal pathways in VLMs are routinely fitted with learnable gates so the optimiser can decide how much of the signal to admit. We find that the optimiser almost always decides on zero: across five injection designs, every gated pathway becomes behaviourally closed, with accuracy invariant to ablating the pathway at inference even when the gate parameter would nominally pass 30-45% of the signal. We attribute this suppression phenomenon to two regimes, a dead-gradient regime formalised through the caption-invariance of image-derived signals, and a negative-utility regime in which the auxiliary signal actively hurts the loss.
